# LFE Lab Notebook

## Project overview
- Goal: using Shelly's USGS LFE catalog for the southern edge of Cascadia near the Mendocino triple junction (27 families, ~40.03-40.09N, -123.53 to -123.71, 22-29 km depth), start with the most active family (then the other 26 if it works):
  1. Download waveforms of every repeat at every nearby station.
  2. Histogram of repeats through time.
  3. Locate the family with the most up-to-date 3D velocity model.
  4. Relative (HypoDD-style) relocation of each event within the family through time.
  5. REDPy/CWIRE-style coda-wave dv/v through time per station.
- A Fable agent is used for methods advice when needed.
- Project root: /home/seismic/LFE. Python env: conda `lfe` at /home/seismic/anaconda3/envs/lfe (do not use base; its h5py is broken).

## Current status / next steps
- Status (2026-11-22 22:45): all 27 family pipelines and the seven science investigations are complete. Report v9 published (same URL, https://claude.ai/artifact/Vrvac77ndqzBi6v1hDdVcU) with a "What we learned" synthesis at the top:
  - New: only the M7 lowered pore pressure at the LFE fault (undrained dp) and only it silenced the system; frictional state unchanged (tidal sensitivity, sizes, no healing; moment repaid in 60-90 d; sigma_eff >~6 kPa, rough); resolved small patches ~560x350 m along strike in Shelly's plane; inter-family coupling (~9x at 10 min, stronger along strike) and inward sweeps at 80-100 km/d, unchanged by the M7; an annual LFE-rate cycle of 5-7% peaking Aug-Sep, in phase with dv/v; the 18-yr LFE-coda dv/v record (annual cycle, shaking-scaled coseismic drops, non-healing decline since 2021).
  - Confirms: M7 cessation (Shelly & Materna 2026); NNE-dipping ~45 deg plane; strong linear tidal modulation (Shelly et al. 2026).
  - Not supported: healing; source migration at the M7/since 2020 and inter-family migration >1 m/yr; a deep velocity change (only the early-coda 60-d transient remains, 2.5 sigma, confounded); in-patch fronts (overlap pull ~50 m), tidal location shifts, a propagating restart; remote triggering (needs a waveform check; SUPERSEDED 2026-11-23: waveform check supports it, tentative).
- Round 4 done (2026-11-23): GNSS, patch images, SPOTL tides + poroelastic test, source spectra, SWS, dv/v instrument check. Inter-family spectral ratios DONE 2026-11-23 (scripts/sci_family_spectra.py). DV/V decline depth test DONE 2026-11-23 (top few hundred metres best supported; km-scale disfavoured, not excluded). Matched-filter scan around Petrolia/Ferndale/M7 DONE 2026-11-23 ~17:00 (data/scan_quakes/; only the M7 is a robust multi-day cessation; Petrolia/Ferndale 1-2 d dips are partly detection loss and within LFE burstiness; Ferndale 30-d halving = storm/aftershock detection loss; see "Findings: quake matched-filter scans"). (source-receiver dv/v separation DONE 2026-11-23: family source terms null, deep common term bounded only). Deep vs shallow dv/v separation DONE 2026-11-23: deep crust unchanged to ~+/-0.02%, changes are receiver-side upper crust. [SUPERSEDED/WITHDRAWN (see 2026-11-25 code review)] Source-region dv/v from S-P times DONE 2026-11-24: within 10 km of the LFEs within ~+/-0.3% (95%), no Vp/Vs change. Full-record SC noise dv/v for 26 other stations DONE 2026-11-25 (data/sci/noise_dvv_sc_full/). On-disk band noise dv/v for ~38 stations DONE 2026-11-24 (data/sci/noise_dvv_bands_ondisk/). Long-period inter-station noise dv/v DONE 2026-11-25 (no deep change; see its own section). Full-record SC noise dv/v (27 stations, 5 bands) DONE 2026-11-25. Running: Ducellier Phase 2 scan. Depth summary built (21 observables); report v39 published; GitHub synced (commit d1ff4b7). Joint 3-D / multi-dataset dv/v inversion DONE 2026-11-25 16:30 (see its PRODUCTION RESULTS section: no deep change detected; travel-time 2-sigma detectability ~0.2-1% depending on target; -0.1% deep change not testable; shallow-only model fits all data). Attribution (sci_dvv_attr_*) production DONE 2026-11-25 ~16:50 (see its section; report v43, GitHub 100de3e). Ducellier Phase 2 scan running (ETA ~Nov 26 evening). - Resolved: injection under-recovery (~70%) explained (lapse/frequency-dependent recovery: late coda and 4-8 Hz recover only 33-47% vs 70-78% for early coda and 2-4 Hz); optional: correct step amplitudes by band-specific recovery factors.
- Plan (2026-11-23 ~07:50): the user authorised everything ("do what you have to progress", "ok on everything"), including installing SPOTL and the heavy template-matching compute. The machine is shared with another account (~45 cores busy); the user is fine with us using it. Six investigations launched (each will be logged when reviewed):
  1. Shear-wave splitting through time (scripts/sci_sws.py, data/sci/sws/).
  2. Source spectra / corner frequency through time and across the quakes (scripts/sci_source_spectra.py, data/sci/source_spectra/).
  3. GNSS: slow slip vs LFE activity, M7 postseismic at LFE depth, velocity changes after 2021 (scripts/sci_gnss.py, data/sci/gnss/).
  4. Sharper patch images: AxB-consistent subset, deconvolution, synthetic recovery tests (scripts/sci_patch_images.py, data/sci/patch_images/).
  5. SPOTL ocean-tide loading at LFE depth, then refit tidal slip sense, mu' and a*sigma. SPOTL is installed in tools/spotl (scripts/sci_tidal_ocean_spotl.py, data/sci/tidal_spotl/).
  6. Our own matched-filter scan, all families, around Petrolia and Ferndale (and the M7): real rate change vs detection loss (data/scan_quakes/).
- Planned sequence: (1) alignment QC, (2) master stack + 1D/3D location vs Shelly, (3) sub-stack relocation on fixed station subset, (4) dv/v null tests, (5) scale to 27 families.

## Velocity model & travel times
- 2026-11-21 Model: Furlong et al. 2024 Tectonics; data release Benz 2024 doi 10.5066/P14QGYIY (ScienceBase 65d78013d34ec3e1801d7ec8). P and S models + ray coverage (pm/sm/pc/sc.llzv.gz) in data/velocity/furlong2024/.
  - Regular grid in transverse Mercator (lon_0 -122.7, lat_0 40.4, km): 3 x 3 x 0.5 km, 120x174x150 nodes, z -3.75..70.75 km. Starting model vel_p.1d with Vs = Vp/1.78.
- 2026-11-21 At family 23: Vp 7.27 km/s at 27 km (fast; consistent with captured Pioneer fragment); P ray coverage >1000 km/cell; S coverage weak (20-35 km/cell), Vp/Vs ~1.76-1.78 ~ starting value, so the S model is essentially Vp/1.78 (caveat).
- 2026-11-21 scripts/traveltimes_3d.py: PyKonal point-source eikonal, 0.5 km grid, +/-80 km, z -2..45 km; station z = -elev + sensor depth; saves sub-volume +/-15 km, z 12-42 km around family in data/velocity/tt_3d/{NET.STA}.npz + grid.json. 3D run done for all 48 stations in 5.4 min (16 procs).
- 2026-11-21 1D baseline (Furlong vel_p.1d) to follow; Yoon & Shelly 1D not available locally.
- 2026-11-21 1D baseline travel times done (Furlong starting model vel_p.1d, Vs=Vp/1.78), same grid/script -> data/velocity/tt_1d/ (48 stations). Logs: logs/tt_3d.log, logs/tt_1d.log.
- 2026-11-21 Sanity check, travel times from Shelly's family-23 centroid (27 km), 3D / 1D:
  - BK.JEWT: P 5.19/4.97 s, S 9.24/8.85 s
  - NC.KMR: P 6.31/6.06 s, S 11.22/10.79 s
  - BK.BRIC: P 7.09/6.93 s, S 12.57/12.33 s
  - PB.B934: P 6.38/6.42 s, S 11.30/11.44 s
  - PB.B046 (64 km): P 11.98/12.07 s, S 21.20/21.49 s
  - 3D-1D differences up to ~0.4 s in S, so model choice matters at the ~1 km level. S-P (3D) is 4.0 s at JEWT to 9.2 s at B046.
## Findings: master stacks (test subset 2008 to mid-2010, ~2,000 events/channel, family 23)
- 2026-11-21 Master stacks SNR 25-100 at most channels; clear S on all stations, P visible on several verticals (NC.KMR, NC.KIP, NC.KCR, PB.B049, PB.B046). Figure: figures/fam23_master_stacks_test.png.
- 2026-11-21 Observed S arrivals are systematically later than 3D-predicted from Shelly's centroid when anchored as "catalog time = first-station S - 1 s": ~1.5 s late at nearest stations, growing to ~3 s at ~60 km. Moveout is larger than predicted (location and/or velocity difference; origin time is a free parameter in the location anyway). To be resolved by location.
- 2026-11-21 Problem channels: NC.KPP..SHZ and NC.KCPB..HHN/HHE dominated by monochromatic noise (KPP's anomalously high median CC 0.32 comes from this); NC.KBS..SHZ stack SNR 2 (likely dead/problem in 2008-2010). Exclude/flag these.
- 2026-11-21 Preliminary location (test-subset 2008 to mid-2010 templates; to be redone with full dataset, which adds BK broadbands and newer NC 3-component stations):
  - 3D (Furlong): 40.055N, -123.544W, 25.5 km; 1.05 km from Shelly centroid, dz -1.5 km; bootstrap sd ~0.95/0.66 km horizontal, 1.6 km depth; 10 S + 9 P picks; L1 misfit 0.14 s; origin ~ catalog time - 8.46 s.
  - 1D baseline: 40.041N, -123.561W, 27.0 km; 1.44 km from Shelly; sd ~0.85/1.14 km, 1.8 km depth; L1 0.13 s.
  - Both pass the <=2 km agreement criterion. Depth poorly resolved (misfit valley elongated in depth).
  - Vp/Vs scan (S travel times scaled by f=1.00-1.12): prefers f=1.00 (3D) and 1.02 (1D), weakly constrained with only 10 S picks.
- 2026-11-21 Relative relocation (test subset 2008 to mid-2010):
  - Bins (15 x 150 events): centroid stable to +/-~35 m, bootstrap sd 30-50 m.
  - Events: 644 of 2,282 (28%) pass (>=6 sta, CC>=0.5, gap<180 deg); median sd ~0.4 km horizontal, ~0.8 km depth; IQR of offsets +/-~250 m (consistent with Fable estimate 200-500 m).
  - CAVEAT: CC>=0.5 vs master selects events near the master, so true spread is under-estimated (selection bias).
- 2026-11-21 Yearly lag QC (integer-sample version): all lags 0 at 0.02 s resolution for 2008-2010; no gross timing problems. Re-run with sub-sample lags on full data.
- 2026-11-21 dv/v test (100-event bins, only 8 bins in subset):
  - Best channels PB.B047, PB.B049, PB.B933, PB.B935 (master SNR>=15); coda only 3-5 s after S (weak LFE coda; caveat).
  - Bin-to-bin dv/v scatter 0.02-0.04% (coda) vs 0.24-0.6% on the pre-event-noise control; synthetic -0.10% recovered as -0.098 to -0.102%.
- 2026-11-21 Isolation counts (family 23, v2, all-family catalog): +/-5 s 21,951; +/-10 s 11,944; +/-15 s 7,939 (32%); +/-20 s 5,987; +/-30 s 4,091; about 40 bins of 200 at +/-15 s.
- 2026-11-21 Validation with earthquakes (scripts/validate_location_eq.py): 31 NCSN events M>=1.5, 2012-2026, inside the location sub-volume (data/validation/ncsn_events_box.txt); NCSN picks fetched as QuakeML with arrivals (cached data/validation/quakeml/); relocated with our grids (L1, free origin; P sigma 0.05 s, S sigma 0.10 s).
  - 3D: 26 events off grid edge; horizontal offset vs NCSN median 1.75 km; depth systematically DEEPER than NCSN by median +2.54 km (IQR 2.24-3.68); origin -0.35 s; L1 0.085 s.
  - 1D (Furlong vel_p.1d): 24 events; dh median 0.91 km; dz median +3.80 km (IQR 3.47-5.35); L1 0.111 s.
  - Interpretation: 3D fits earthquake picks better (lower L1), so 3D is primary. Both models put events deeper than NCSN (NCSN uses a 1D model + its own station corrections); absolute depth is the least certain quantity and this cannot show which is biased.
  - Station residuals (candidate statics; data/validation/station_residuals_{3d,1d}.csv): 3D P medians mostly within +/-0.13 s (largest BK.HAYF +0.22, BK.GHOP +0.13, PB.B047 +0.11); S up to ~+0.3 s (BK.BRIC, BK.GHOP, NC.KTJ, PB.B047), PB.B934 S -0.18.
- 2026-11-21 Location with statics (test subset; locate_family.py gained --statics, subtracting median earthquake residuals with >=5 picks):
  - 3D + statics: 40.055N, -123.556W, 28.0 km; 0.14 km from Shelly centroid, dz +1.0 km; sd 0.87/0.77 km horizontal, 2.0 km depth; s_scale 1.02; L1 0.17 s.
  - 1D + statics: 40.046N, -123.555W, 27.5 km; 0.87 km from Shelly; sd 1.5/1.6 km horizontal, 3.0 km depth.
## Findings: end-to-end PARTIAL run, family 23 (download at ~mid-2014; 22:18-22:28 PST)
All stages ran without error. Preliminary; to be superseded by the full run.
- 2026-11-21 Histogram figures/fam23_repeats_through_time.png: 24,718 v2 repeats, 2008-02..2026-06; typically 80-180/month; 2008 low (network); 2018-2021 ~50-100/month; late-2021-2022 150-220/month; dips Jan 2023 and Dec 2024.
- 2026-11-21 Activity around mainshocks (v2):
  - M7.0 Mendocino 2024-12-05: last LFE 16 h before, next 10.3 days after; ~11-day gap is the longest in 2009-2026 (99th pct gap 2.9 d); sporadic singles until full resumption ~23 days after (19 LFEs on 2024-12-28); 63 LFEs in 30 d after vs 115 before. Consistent with Shelly & Materna 2026 (eastern families off longest, ~25 d).
  - M6.4 Ferndale 2022-12-20: first LFE 3.5 d after (within normal); 30-d count 67 vs 119 before (suppressed ~1 month).
  - M6.1 Petrolia 2021-12-20: first LFE 3.4 d after, but none ~2 d before either; 149 vs 188; no clear shutdown.
- 2026-11-21 Location (partial, 3D + statics): 40.050N, -123.555W, depth 31.0 km; 0.37 km horizontal from Shelly; sd 0.73/0.65 km horizontal, 1.5 km depth; 10 S + 13 P. Depth unstable across subsets (25.5-31 km); depth is the weak direction.
- 2026-11-21 Relocation bins (59 x 150 events, 2008-2014): centroid stable within +/-~30 m (sd ~20 m); possible ~30 m shallower depth 2012-2013 (marginal).
- 2026-11-21 Relocation events: 3,489 of 8,801 (40%) relocated; median sd ~0.42 km horizontal, ~0.75 km depth; best 1,569 (sd<0.5 km horizontal, <1 km depth) form an elongated ~1 km WNW-ESE cloud aligned with Shelly et al. 2026 fault strike 293 deg, independent of any assumed geometry. figures/fam23_relocations_3d.png.
- 2026-11-21 dv/v (40 bins defined; 15 with data through 2014; 200 isolated events each): 17 channels pass (master SNR>=15), mostly PB boreholes + NC.KMR, NC.KMPB; coda 3-10 s (longest at PB boreholes); bin scatter 0.012-0.06% vs noise-window control 0.34-0.63%; synthetic -0.10% recovered as -0.099..-0.100% on all channels.
- 2026-11-21 Station detections (partial data, 2008 to mid-2014): events have data at median 21 stations but are individually detected (1% FAR) at a median of only 1 station. Best stations detect 16-19% of repeats (NC.KMR 17.5 km, PB.B049, PB.B047, NC.KMPB, PB.B935); most others 2-8%. Individual LFEs are mostly below single-station detectability; Shelly's network-summed CC detects them; stacks are required for location/relocation/dv/v.
- 2026-11-21 Fixed-station (core set) bin relocation: 51 of 59 bins; spread +/-30 m E, +/-19 m N, +/-22 m depth; same small 2012-2013 shallowing (~-14 to -28 m) as the all-station run, so no evidence of network-change artifacts through 2014.
- 2026-11-21 dv/v sub-bands and split halves (partial data 2008 to mid-2014): 4-8 Hz much noisier (coda CC ~0.6, scatter ~0.25%) than 2-8 Hz; LFE energy is mostly below ~5 Hz. Split halves: all-pairs inversion becomes unstable with half-size bins (std up to ~0.1%) while the vs-reference series stays robust (~0.02%).
- 2026-11-21 Network-average dv/v (17 channels: NC.KMPB.HHE, NC.KMR.HHZ, PB.B045, B046, B047, B049, B932, B933, B935 components): within +/-0.018% over 2008-2014, per-bin errors 0.004-0.01%; direct-S dt within +/-5 ms (no clock problems). Preliminary (partial data).

No significant jump at any mainshock (the all-station M7 dN -13+/-6 m did not survive the fixed-station test).

- 2026-11-22 dv/v coarse (200 isolated +/-15 s events/bin, 40 bins, 28 channels): within +/-0.02%; apparent -0.038% drop across M7 (Sep 2024 +0.020+/-0.009 to Feb 2025 -0.018+/-0.008), but consecutive non-earthquake bins swing similarly and look seasonal.
- 2026-11-22 dv/v fine bins: dvv_family.py --iso 5 --nbin 150 (21,951 isolated events, 147 bins) + odd/even; scripts/dvv_fit.py: network average over fixed channel list (mean coda CC>=0.7, noise >=5x coda scatter) with split-half errors from the SAME channels; WLSQ fit offset + trend + annual + steps; bootstrap.
  - RESULT (14 channels, 145 bins 2008-11..2026-06, median err 0.015%, reduced chi2 1.4): annual amplitude 0.0076% (cos term 3.3 sigma), fastest ~day 215 (early Aug), slowest ~early Feb (wet-season groundwater signature); trend -0.001%/yr (1.9 sigma).
  - Steps: Petrolia +0.0099+/-0.0064% (1.6 sigma), Ferndale +0.0013+/-0.006% (0.2 sigma), M7 -0.0066+/-0.0054% (1.2 sigma). NO significant coseismic dv/v at any mainshock; 2-sigma bound <~0.015-0.02%. Coarse-bin fit also null.
  - Figures: figures/fam23_dvv_fit_iso5_n150.png, figures/fam23_dvv_network.png.
- 2026-11-22 dv/v post-M7 transient check (fine bins):
  - Visual: 4-5 fine bins right after the M7 (Jan-Mar 2025) at about -0.025 to -0.04%, below the step fit and below the same season in other years, recovering by mid-2025.
  - Transient test (deseasoned model + exponential-recovery term per mainshock, tau grid 20-365 d or permanent step; null = trend+annual, chi2 202.9 for 145 bins, reduced chi2 ~1.4): M7 best tau 60 d, amplitude -0.061 +/- 0.022% (-2.8 sigma), delta chi2 = 11 vs null (tau chosen from grid, so look-elsewhere inflates significance). Petrolia best = permanent step +0.0097 +/- 0.0043% (+2.2 sigma, delta chi2 7.0; sign unexpected); Ferndale step +0.0056 +/- 0.0041% (1.4 sigma).
  - Empirical test: deseasoned residual of the 3 bins within 100 d after M7 (2025-01-03..03-06) = -0.0226%; same calendar window in 16 other years: mean -0.0002%, sd 0.0139%, min -0.0312% (2012); z = -1.6, 1 of 16 years lower.
  - VERDICT: possible transient post-M7 velocity drop of a few hundredths of a percent recovering over ~2 months; 2-3 sigma model-dependent, within interannual variability empirically, so suggestive, not established. Best test: the other 26 families (same stations, independent sources; a real medium change must appear in all simultaneously).
- 2026-11-22 Silent period (ideas 1, 3, 5): silent_period.py on full data + CONTROL (fake split 2024-08-19, late 2024-10-20; tag _control).
  - (1) Matched-filter amplitudes: pre 1.161+/-0.038, restart 0.997+/-0.023, late 0.929+/-0.021 (ratio 0.86; control ratio 1.00). First 25 restart events 1.20x pre (95% CI 0.96-1.44) vs control first 25 0.81x (0.66-0.97), so +/-20% swings occur by chance; first 50: 1.08 (0.93-1.25); first 100: 0.96. Shape-independent check (group-mean S energy minus noise energy, 2-8 Hz horizontals): restart/pre 1.12 (CI 0.98-1.37), late 0.89 (0.76-1.07); control 1.14 (0.59-1.36); the 14% matched-filter decrease is NOT confirmed (likely slight waveform-shape change or noise). CONCLUSION: no robust amplitude change; no healing signal, no robust decrease.
  - (3) 101 of 168 shifts pass (S 88, P 13); d(tS-tP) restart vs pre at 6 stations: GHOP +12.9, SPRL +10.8, USAL +12.7, KMR +10.5, HUNT -4.1, B049 -5.0 ms; mean +6.3+/-3.5 ms vs control -2.6+/-2.6 ms (~2 sigma). No significant Vp/Vs change (bound ~0.3%); if anything opposite sign to the pore-pressure-drop prediction.
  - (5) SH/SV and P/S: real M7 3 of 25 stations |z|>2 (SH/SV), 3 of 20 (P/S); control 4 of 26 and 4 of 21, consistent with natural variability. No rake/mechanism change. Large changes at BK.BRIC (SH/SV 3.64 to 1.55; HHE flagged low quality), BK.BJES, BK.HALS likely instrument issues.
- 2026-11-22 Idea 6 (reverse motion) scan results (era templates, 73 channels; output data/scan/fam23_scan_all.csv):
  - Pre-M7 (Nov 10-Dec 5, 25.8 d): 103 catalog f23 events, 95 scan positives (65 catalog f23, 3 other fam, 27 new), recall 63%; 41 negatives (10 at f23 events = misclassified, 5 at other fam, 26 isolated). False-reversal fraction among catalog-matched 0.133; isolated reversed ~1.0/day ~ new positives (27), i.e. symmetric noise false alarms at the 8xMAD threshold.
  - Silence (Dec 5-28, 22.2 d): 26 catalog (from Dec 16), 26 positives (14 catalog, 2 other, 10 new), recall 54%; 19 negatives (1 at f23 event, 18 isolated ~0.8/day, all cc/thr 1.00-1.08, |meancc| 0.09-0.12), same as noise rate.
  - Dec 5-16 (before first catalog LFE): only 4 positives, all at threshold, so the family is truly quiet, not just below catalog threshold. Restart bursts Dec 23 strong (up to 2.5x threshold).
  - Clusters of reversed candidates on Dec 21 (5) and Dec 27 (3) being checked (per-channel coherence + NCSN earthquakes; scripts/check_reversed.py, running).
  - Post (Dec 28-Jan 10): 72 catalog, 51 positives, recall 49%, 32 negatives (19 at f23 events, 13 isolated).
- 2026-11-22 Idea 6 verdict (scripts/check_reversed.py -> data/scan/fam23_coherence_check.csv): per-channel CC at each detection (era templates, +/-0.1 s lag).
  - Group means (frac channels with detection's sign / median |CC| / frac |CC|>0.2): real catalog LFEs (ref, n=25) 0.78 / 0.34 / 0.66; pre-M7 isolated reversed = noise ref (n=26) 0.69 / 0.26 / 0.50; pre-M7 new positives (n=27) 0.63 / 0.24 / 0.41; SILENCE isolated reversed (n=18) 0.65 / 0.22 / 0.40; silence new positives (n=10) 0.64 / 0.25 / 0.43. No silence candidate reaches real-LFE coherence (max 0.73 / 0.28).
  - NCSN catalog: 3,653 events within 1.4 deg in Nov 10-Jan 10 (M7 aftershocks); several candidates follow M1.1-3.5 earthquakes by 37-114 s (incl. Dec 21 and Dec 27 clusters).
  - VERDICT: NO evidence of reverse (left-lateral) slip during the post-M7 silence. Reversed detections occur at the noise rate (0.8/d vs 1.0/d pre-M7) with noise-like coherence; clusters coincide with aftershock activity; new positive detections before Dec 16 are also noise-like, so the family is genuinely silent (not just sub-threshold) until 2024-12-16.
  - Note: frac_same_sign >0.5 even for noise because max-over-lag selection biases sign.
- 2026-11-22 Fixed-station relocation of INDIVIDUAL events (family 23), user request:
  - Run: `scripts/relocate_relative.py --mode events --stations <15 core: PB.B045,B046,B047,B049,B932,B933,B934,B935, NC.KMR,KMPB,KBN,KFP,KIP,KCR,KSM> --minsta 5 --tag _core` -> data/relocations/fam23_events_3d_core.csv (4 min). Log logs/fam23_reloc_events_core.log.
  - 8,652 events relocated, 3,830 well constrained (sd<0.5 km horizontal, <1 km depth), vs all-station run 14,355 / 8,636.
  - Agreement with all-station run (3,555 events well constrained in both): r = 0.88 E, 0.83 N, 0.86 Z. 3,949 of 8,495 common events have identical solutions (their CC>=0.5 observations come only from core stations anyway).
  - Yearly medians agree within a few tens of m 2008-2024; core run drifts ~-50 m north after the M7 (2025-26) while the all-station run does not.
  - Quake step test (median after - before, well-constrained events, vs null of the same statistic at weekly random dates 2009-2025): core run gave apparent Ferndale E +98 m (+/-1 yr, p=0.03) and M7 N -55 m (+/-1 yr, p=0.01); all-station run: nothing (all p>=0.29).
  - Availability around quakes (the 'fixed' network is not fixed in practice): Ferndale: KMPB 97%->36%, B047 46%->99%, KSM 0->28%; M7: KBN 60%->100%, KCR 98->68%, KSM 85->52%, KMR 98->83%. With ~8 stations/event, dropping one station shifts event solutions.

M7 +/-1 yr: N -3 m (p=0.89), E -60 (p=0.43), Z -89 (p=0.20); Ferndale +/-1 yr: E +43 (p=0.70), N -49 (p=0.33), Z -100 (p=0.15). Apparent steps vanish, so they were station-availability artefacts.
  - VERDICT: individual-event fixed-station relocation confirms the bin result: no resolvable source shift at any of the three quakes. Individual-event precision ~50-150 m (null sd of 1-yr medians 25-70 m), so the bin stacks (+5+/-9 m at M7) remain the tightest constraint.

- 2026-11-22 Catalog-only response of all 27 families to all 13 quakes (scripts/quake_response_catalog.py -> data/reports/quake_response_catalog.csv): all-family 30-d count vs preceding-year rate, with a 3000-random-date null.
  - M7: ratio 0.45 (p<0.001); 24/27 families have a top-10 gap since 2009 spanning it; the all-family gap spanning it is 3.63 d (random-date max 4.34 d, 99th percentile 1.7 d).
  - 2022 M6.4 Ferndale: 0.52 (p=0.001), but no long gaps (all-family gap 0.27 d).
  - 2010 M6.5 Eureka (112 km): 0.63 (p=0.015).
  - 2014 M6.8 (154 km): 0.81, and 6 families with a top-10 gap.
  - 2021 Petrolia: 1.32 (p=0.07 for an increase).
  - All the others: 0.73-1.12.
  - Caveat: post-quake rate decreases could be detection loss from aftershock noise raising the matched-filter threshold. Shelly's catalog can't tell these apart; our own reverse/normal scan is only done around the M7.
- 2026-11-22 Fable review of CWIRE 1.0.0 source (file:line refs verified in cwire/inversion.py):
  - CWIRE pairs: all pairs within a family, body-wave CC>0.7, events more than 1800 s apart.
  - Alignment and coda: aligned on P (lag from a window at P-0.25 s); coda window from S+0.25 s, 14 s long; 0.5-15 Hz.
  - Pair measurement: stretch with a free lag (objective 1 - max-over-lag CC); +/-2% coarse search, then 0.04% steps, then a spline minimum; kept only if CC improves.
  - Inversion: pairs with post-stretch CC>0.65, unweighted; evenly spaced control points (default 0.5 h), event times linearly interpolated between control points; 1st- and 2nd-difference regularization 0.5/0.5*lambda, lambda grid 5000..0.5.
  - CORRECTION to the earlier note (max curvature): lambda is chosen as the point farthest from the chord in linear (roughness, chi2) space, NOT maximum curvature.
  - The "mean" row actually uses values 0..n-1 (sum j*m_j = 0), so the absolute level is arbitrary; no breaks at earthquakes; StdErr = residual scatter per segment; one inversion per station/channel, with all families pooled.
  - KEY ISSUE with OUR method: stretch_cc has no free shift and stretches about origin time. A timing error delta maps to dv/v ~ delta/t_lapse; 5 ms at about 15 s ~ 0.03%, the size of the family-23 transient. Catalog-time jitter from network changes could do this.
  - Recommendation (hybrid): stacks of 50 isolated LFEs (25 in the 6 months after each quake), stamped with the mean member time; align on direct S, then stretch relative to S; upsample to 200 Hz; pairs within ~2 yr plus each stack vs reference; CWIRE design matrix with 30-d control points (10 d after quakes), 1st+2nd-difference rows with breaks at the quakes, lambda by leave-channels-out CV; joint inversion over channels and also over all 27 families (pairs only within a family).
  - Test steps/transient by fitting the parametric model directly to the pair data, not to the smoothed curve. Errors: block bootstrap over stacks/bursts, odd/even split, channel/family jackknife. Optional cross-check by calling cwire.velocity.measure_velocity and cwire.inversion.invertvelocity as a library (pass n_proc explicitly, numpy<2).
- 2026-11-22 ~15:00 Family-23 CWIRE-style dv/v validation:
  - Injection of -0.05% after the M7 recovered -0.035% (~70%), transient unchanged, so steps are underestimated by ~30%.
  - Odd/even halves: annual cycle reproduces (0.031%/0.028%, day 252/239); M7 step reproduces (-0.040+/-0.014 / -0.037+/-0.017); the 60-d transient does NOT (-0.077+/-0.040 / -0.045+/-0.050 vs -0.153 with all events).
  - Station subsets: PB borehole: no quake steps (M7 -0.002+/-0.017); annual 0.022%; transient -0.079+/-0.043. BK surface: steps Petrolia -0.080+/-0.027, Ferndale -0.088+/-0.029, M7 -0.106+/-0.025; annual 0.065%. NC: 4 channels. Interpretation: the quake steps and most of the annual cycle are near-surface (receiver side).
  - Noise-window control: no channel passes QC (as expected); the run crashed on empty input; this needs a guard.
  - User pushback: results were being framed too negatively. The signals are real: an 18-yr annual cycle at 5.9 sigma, coseismic drops with recovery, depth information from borehole vs surface. Possible larger annual amplitude after 2022 is still to test.
- 2026-11-22 ~15:00 M7 relocation with the availability-matched station set (M7SET) vs core, depth step (negative = shallower): fam23 -21+/-17 vs -28+/-15; fam8 -8+/-9 vs -13+/-7; fam1 -22+/-3 vs -20+/-7 (only 2-3 bins per side). fam27 and fam24: M7SET relocated 0 bins (cause to investigate). The shallowing is NOT removed by availability matching; <=2 sigma each; to be combined across families.

- 2026-11-22 Pooled CWIRE-style dv/v (scripts/dvv_cwire_pool.py): CWIRE layout, one curve per station-channel pooling all families (pairs within family, a per-family reference offset, lambda by leave-families-out CV, block bootstrap over family-stacks). 27 families, 77 channels, 1.91M rows. Outputs data/dvv/pool/, figures/dvv_pool_map.png, figures/dvv_pool_channels.png.
  - Results (inverse-variance means over channels; errors inflated by sqrt(chi2r)):
    - Surface BK/NC, 36 ch: Petrolia -0.033+/-0.008, Ferndale -0.037+/-0.007, M7 -0.065+/-0.011 (5.8 sigma), 60-d M7 transient -0.110+/-0.017 (6.6 sigma), annual median 0.042%, peak ~day 250.
    - Borehole PB, 22 ch: Petrolia -0.011+/-0.005, Ferndale -0.018+/-0.007, M7 -0.016+/-0.005 (3.3 sigma), transient -0.056+/-0.021 (2.7 sigma), annual 0.018%, peak ~day 258.
  - CORRECTION to the earlier family-23-only conclusions: with all families pooled, the borehole channels DO show significant (smaller) drops and a post-M7 transient. The changes are depth-dependent (surface ~3-4x borehole), not purely near-surface. The post-M7 transient IS significant in the pooled data (it was not robust in family-23 odd/even halves alone).
  - Largest M7 drops: BK.BRIC -0.17 (7 sigma), BK.SPRL -0.15, BK.BJES -0.12, NC.KMPB -0.09. Stations SPRL/USAL/HALS have identical Petrolia and Ferndale steps (their data start after 2021, so collinear); ignore those.

- 2026-11-22 ~17:40 Burst cascades (scripts/burst_cascades.py; Fable idea 1; catalog only):
  - Method: family bursts = events with gaps <=2 h, onset = first event; network episodes = onsets clustered with gaps <=6 h, keeping episodes with >=6 families; per episode, a robust fit of onset time to family E/N position gives a slowness vector, speed and azimuth; null = permute family positions within the episode (200x).
  - Outputs: data/cascades/episodes.csv, figures/burst_cascades.png.
  - Results: 78,113 family bursts; 4,117 episodes; 1,296 (31%) show significant spatial ordering (p<0.05; 5% expected), so bursts do propagate across the array. Median apparent speed 34 km/day (IQR 17-113).

  - The UNDRAINED PORE-PRESSURE DROP (mean-stress dilatation) uniquely separates the M7 in 100% of variants; its least negative dp (-0.8) is below every other quake's most negative (-0.24). Physical reason: the LFEs sit in the dilatational quadrant SE of the eastern tip of the right-lateral M7 rupture, whereas Ferndale and the Gorda-slab quakes compress them.
  - Undrained Coulomb makes the M7 most negative only for mu >~0.61 (B 0.7) or 0.52 (B 0.9), i.e. pore pressure must matter beyond a low-friction Coulomb term.

  - Caveats: factor ~2 in absolute stress; uniform rectangles for most sources.
  - COORDINATOR INFERENCE (rough, model-dependent): combining with the tides result (a >~55% drop in tidal sensitivity after the M7 is excluded) and d(alpha)/alpha ~ -d(sigma_eff)/sigma_eff (rate-state, a*sigma), an effective-normal-stress increase of ~3.5 kPa implies sigma_eff >~6 kPa. Lower bound only; the factor-2 stress uncertainty applies. Agrees with Shelly & Materna that the M7 is the only quake with a pore-pressure drop at the LFEs, and adds that dCFS and clamping alone cannot discriminate.

It does NOT scale with PGV (p=0.46); borehole ~ surface. Candidates: diffusive re-pressurisation from the west/updip, a geometry/source-size effect, or rate normalisation (the ratio depends on each family's background rate). Undetermined. Refit on unsmoothed monthly bins: decline PB -0.056% (z -5.8, not -18); surface -0.116% (z -7.4, not -11). Earlier z-values for steps/timing from the pooled curves must be revised down (the dv/v placebo calibration's long-term z used the curves).
  1. Station vs family: no family effect (F_fam 0.64 vs placebo 1.15-2.50; z -2.2). Families in two PB groups correlate rho -0.13 (placebo 0.17+/-0.24). Detectable family SD >=0.06%. Families are within ~15 km, so this mainly tests the source side. Station effect F 11.5 (z +5), driven by KMPB and B045. So the decline is RECEIVER-SIDE, not at the LFE sources.
  2. Surface - borehole -0.060+/-0.018% (z -3.3; ratio 2.1). PB boreholes homogeneous. Surface: KMPB -0.24, BRIC -0.19, RBOW -0.12, RVIT -0.10, KMR -0.05, BJES -0.05, HAYF 0.00. No robust location/distance pattern.
  3. Timing: no change before Petrolia. PB: staircase ~ ramp from mid-2021 (dAIC 1). Surface: steps preferred (dAIC 13-15), dominated by the M7 drop -0.10% (z -4.3); Petrolia/Ferndale level changes n.s. Inter-quake slopes n.s.: "kept declining after each quake" and "declined between Ferndale and the M7" are NOT supported (CORRECTION). PB was already -0.023% in 2022 H2, before the 2023 data-source switch.
  4. Hydrology (Eureka GHCN USW00024213): short-timescale precipitation proxies explain the pre-2021 monthly signal better than a sinusoid (R^2 0.40 vs 0.25), so the ANNUAL CYCLE IS LIKELY HYDROLOGICAL (new). The 2023-25 wet years are not unprecedented (3580 mm vs 3645 in WY2015-17). The pre-2021-calibrated model predicts only -0.01 to -0.03% for 2021->2025; scaling to the full decline predicts pre-2021 swings 2.5-7x too large. So rainfall explains <=~1/3.
  5. Instruments: PB single epoch 2008-now, unchanged; KMR swap 2025-09 and posthole 2026-03 come after the decline; KMPB epochs 2019/2023 without jumps; BK reinstalls May-Jun 2024: only BRIC HHN jumps (-0.09%, one component).
  - VERDICT: the decline is real, receiver/near-surface, quake-tied (2021-24), not source-side, not rainfall, not instruments. Site damage vs regional shallow change is undetermined, and the shaking link is not shown.

One-bit + whitening (1-Hz smoothed whitening for autocorrelations), notching of persistent spectral lines (69% of 1-4 Hz band at KMPB), 3 cross-component + 3 auto pairs, 1-4 and 2-8 Hz, stretching 2-20 s coda vs 2019-Nov 2021 reference; 5-d block bootstrap; ~200 placebo dates pre-2021 + same-date-other-years.
  - Long-term (mean 2025 - mean 2021, %; noise 1-4 / 2-8 Hz vs LFE coda): KMPB -0.27/-0.33 vs -0.24; BRIC -0.17/-0.28 vs -0.19; B045 -0.32/-0.58 vs -0.13; HAYF -0.01/+0.04 vs 0.00. Placebo-year differences 2019->20, 2020->21 within +/-0.07%; decline starts 2022.
  - Monthly noise vs LFE dv/v: r 0.61-0.75 (0.52-0.72 deseasoned) at KMPB/BRIC/B045; ~0.2 at HAYF.
  - Steps (30 d; placebo sd ~0.05%): M7 BRIC -0.29/-0.64 (partial recovery 2025), B045 -0.34/-0.75 (not recovered 2026), KMPB -0.08/-0.06 (not significant), HAYF inconsistent (cross vs auto disagree in sign). Ferndale B045 -0.26/-0.43 (z -5/-9, recovered mid-2023), BRIC -0.18, KMPB -0.13/-0.29. Petrolia B045 -0.20 (z -4), BRIC -0.09/-0.25, KMPB -0.13/-0.09. KMPB: gradual decline from 2022, -0.39% at 1-4 Hz by 2026, not a jump at the M7.
  - Annual cycle 0.04-0.13% (largest BRIC), dry-season velocity peak (KMPB late May, BRIC mid-Jul, HAYF Sep, B045 Nov); LFE coda peak Sep-Nov, 0.04-0.07%.
  - Robustness (4 processing variants, 1-4 Hz long-term): BRIC -0.08 to -0.19, B045 -0.23 to -0.39, HAYF -0.02 to +0.02, KMPB -0.11 to -0.95 (no-notch variant unreliable).
  - Caveats: gaps (KMPB none at NCEDC Feb-Oct 2023; B045 patchy Aug 2024-Jan 2025, only ~13 post-M7 days; HAYF missing Feb-May 2025). B045 (217 m borehole) has the LARGEST noise change: 1-8 Hz noise samples the top ~1 km regardless of sensor depth, so the "2x larger at surface" LFE pattern is not reproduced. Same sensor as the LFE coda, so instrument drift not fully excluded; the flat HAYF control and quake-timed steps argue against it.
  - INTERPRETATION: the post-2021 LFE-coda dv/v decline and its station pattern are independently reproduced by ambient noise, which has no LFE source, so the decline is a real shallow-crust velocity change near KMPB/BRIC/B045 (not an LFE-source or relocation artefact), consistent with the receiver-side conclusion. Noise steps at BRIC/B045 for all three quakes are clearer than in LFE coda.

  - 200 largest thin-catalogue episodes (median 84 LFEs, 21 families, 3.9 h); PBO B045, B933, B934, B935; per window: trend + 5 tides + barometric fit over +/-15 d with core masked; step/ramp over the episode; null from 32,000 random windows (16,874 clean); joint potency inversion with Okada Green's functions (point source 28 km, 293/40 RL; unit gauge coupling, uncalibrated).
  - Result: NULL. Clean stack (132 episodes) potency 5.0e4 m^3 (boot95 -7.3e4 to 1.7e5), p=0.70 vs null; all 200: p=0.76; 1-day ramp variants p=0.29-0.65.
  - Upper bound per average episode: 1.9e5 m^3 (Mw 4.4; 1-d ramp Mw 4.6), ~180x the LFE moment per episode (Mw-equivalent 2.9). Single-episode noise ~Mw 5.0. Loading budget (2.5 cm/yr on 15x5 km released only in episodes) needs ~1.25e4 m^3 per episode, 15x below the bound. Equivalent slip bound: 19 mm (1 km patch), 2.1 mm (3 km), 0.8 mm (5 km).
  - Conclusion: PBO strainmeters cannot detect the aseismic slip that LFE episodes plausibly accompany; they only exclude episodes with >~180x the LFE moment. Not informative on the slow-slip/LFE moment ratio.
  - Additional details: only B045, B933, B934 and B935 have Gladwin strainmeters; B046, B047, B049 and B932 have none. Data source: EarthScope FDSN (public, no token used) for the RS1-4 10-min gauge channels (T0) and the RDO barometer (TS), 2008-2026, cached in data/sci/strain/raw/. NCEDC has no strain data.
  - Null windows: integer-day shifts with M2 phase within 18 deg. The clean subset excludes windows where teleseisms or regional quakes produce more than 0.3 nstrain.
  - Areal strain at B045 (+4.5 nstrain, CI 1.6-7.5) and B933 (+3.9, CI 1.8-6.0) is slightly above the null band: uncorrected p 0.02-0.03, about 0.3 after correcting for 12 tests. It is absent in the 1-day-ramp variant and is not explained by tidal leakage or season. Its pattern does not match right-lateral slip at 28 km and would need Mw 5 or larger, so it is treated as noise.
  - Geometry sensitivity (data/sci/strain/geometry_sensitivity.csv): dip 90, depth 25-31 km, +/-7 km shifts and reverse slip keep the bound within Mw 4.35-4.64.
  - Gladwin in-situ coupling is usually above 1, so the unit-coupling bound is probably conservative by up to about 2x.
  - Script stages: download, analyse, geometry. The script reuses the Okada code in sci_dose_response.py.

  - Every family is a single compact peak; no multi-asperity or ring structure is visible at this resolution. Raw std is 215-315 m along strike and 230-455 m down-dip, versus true 100-210 m, so the raw density is the true patch blurred by location error. The diagonal elongation of several raw clouds (8, 12, 19, 21) follows the error ellipse, not the AxB patch orientation.
  - Before vs after the M7 (50% and 90% contours): shape and size are unchanged in all families. Centroid shifts are mostly <2 sigma. The largest: fam 2 down-dip -67+/-22 m, fam 23 -23+/-7, fam 20 -40+/-14, fam 19 along-strike +23+/-8, fam 4 along-strike +75+/-31. Down-dip shifts are mostly negative (shallower), matching the known common-mode network-change artefact, so no family-specific change is claimed.
  - Added to the report (v19).
- 2026-11-23 ~08:10 GNSS SEARCH FOR ASEISMIC SLIP AT THE LFEs (scripts/sci_gnss.py; stages download, prep, q1, q2, q3, figs, summary; outputs data/sci/gnss/: summary.json, q1-q3_summary.json; figures figures/sci_gnss_{map,q1_rate_peaks,q2_postseismic,q3_velocity}.png). Verdict: NO robust detection; bounds Mw ~5.1-5.6.
  - Data: NGL IGS20 daily tenv3 plus steps.txt (public, no token). 56 stations within 110 km (P164 at 8.8 km; 8 within 30 km). Common mode: 100 far stations (150-400 km, at least 200 km from the M7). Per-station trajectory model (offsets, seasonal terms, log terms and velocity breaks at the three quakes), then removal of the far-station daily median. Near-station noise 1.0/1.06/3.3 mm (E/N/U).
  - Patch: RL slip on strike 293 / dip 40, 16 x 15.6 km, 22-32 km depth (Okada imported from sci_dose_response.py). Scale: Mw 4.9 moves P164 ~0.1 mm; Mw 5.6 ~1 mm.
  - Q1, multi-week LFE-rate peaks: 20 largest 15-d peaks (2.0-2.8x, ~410 extra LFEs each, mostly Aug-Oct). Stacked potency -0.2 +/- 1.0 e6 m^3 (placebo p 0.80; season-matched 0.97). 95% bound Mw 5.1 for the average peak; single peak ~Mw 5.5. Continuous regression of the patch projection on the cumulative excess LFE count (2009-2026): k = 86 +/- 108 m^3 per LFE (circular-shift p 0.38). 95% bound <300 m^3 per LFE, ~40x the seismic potency of an LFE (7.5 m^3 at Mw 1.5; factor ~5.6 uncertainty from the magnitude). Bound is 340 without the post-M7 year. So aseismic/seismic ratio on the patch < ~40 if slip tracks LFE activity.
  - Q2, M7 postseismic: clearly present regionally (above placebo from days 7-10; P159 ~14 E / 11 N mm, P157 ~-6 E / 14 N mm at 300-365 d). Stations within 30 km moved ~1 mm N by days 5-25 and ~2 mm N by 1-1.5 yr. No feature at the restart (~18 d) or at 60-90 d. Inversion: M7 afterslip components + translation + LFE patch; errors from 106 placebo dates. LFE patch at 60-90 d: -1.3/+0.5 e6 m^3 (z -0.25/+0.15). At 10-18 d: z ~-2 (p 0.047), but this fails: 20 epochs tested, wrong sign for RL, and 33-38% of shifted patches give larger values. The best fake patches lie 50-60 km N (leftover regional postseismic signal). The patch estimate depends on the model and is meaningful only with an afterslip term. 95% bound Mw 5.5-5.6 (3-90 d), Mw 5.7 (1-1.7 yr), about 1.3 yr of plate-rate loading on the patch. The LFE deficit at day 25 (~660 LFEs ~ 5e3 m^3) is ~1500x below the bound.
  - Q3, velocity change after 2021: patch slip-rate change -27 to +62 mm/yr, all |z| <=1.7, with the sign flipping between variants. 95% limits 35-200 mm/yr (1.4-8x plate rate); with a gradient term 16-175. Stations within 30 km moved ~+1-2 mm/yr further N after Ferndale and the M7 (z 2-5), but this is regional and not LFE-specific. Rolling 2-yr patch velocity before 2021 scatters +/-8.6 mm/yr; it does not track the 2-yr LFE rate (r -0.35, p 0.28). Windows spanning the M7 reach -40 mm/yr (postseismic leakage). So no detectable loading change below ~plate rate.
  - Caveats: fixed patch geometry (a larger patch gives similar bounds); simple afterslip model (chi2/dof 1.5-2.5); residual near-network common mode; P156 (ends Apr 2026) and P315 anomalous (handled by robust weights); counts used as a moment proxy.

- 2026-11-23 ~11:30 PATCH IMAGING BY DECONVOLUTION (scripts/sci_patch_images.py; stages synth, real, selbias, time, combine, plot; env OVL=incl|excl; outputs data/sci/patch_images/ovl_incl/ and ovl_excl/, plus population_structure.csv; figures figures/sci_patch_images_{resolution,synthetic_fam23/8/24,families_B_full,families_A_het,kurtosis,time_m7,time_earlylate}.png (+ _excl)).
  - Best method, B_full: Richardson-Lucy/EM deconvolution of the all-station locations. Per-event kernel = sqrt(lambda_i) * Lf * u. lambda_i comes from the formal sd, with the exponent fitted by ML to D = (A-B)/2. Lf comes from the AxB moment identity (cov(G) - C_true). u is the empirical whitened D error shape. Iteration count is chosen by 2-fold burst-grouped cross-validation. Cross-checks: A_het (midpoint of halves), A_emp (purely empirical kernel), consistency subset, raw KDE, AxB excess kurtosis. Run with overlap events included and excluded (other family within +/-10 s; removes 5-65% of events).
  - Methods finding: real location errors are NOT Gaussian. They have a sharper core and heavier tails: 34-54% within r^2 < 0.5 vs 22% for a Gaussian, and 2.5-6% beyond r^2 > 9 vs 1.1%. Gaussian kernels give false sharpness; empirical-shape kernels fix it.
  - Synthetic recovery: B_full is best (fidelity 0.79-0.93 vs 0.33-0.67 raw; 0% false two-peaks under a Gaussian truth). Two blobs: resolved 100% at 200 m separation, 60-80% at 150 m; raw density needs ~300 m. Ring and streak are recovered. Core+halo cannot be told from a Gaussian. Resolution limit ~110 m sd along strike, 85-90 m down dip.
  - ARTEFACT: a uniform disc returns edge "horns" (dip ~0.35). So a dip of 0.2-0.5 means a flat-topped or bounded patch; only a dip > ~0.6 means separate sub-patches.
  - Consistency selection is BIASED: it inflates AxB covariance, and the subset's own D underestimates error by ~2x. So consistency-selected images cannot be calibrated.
  - Real families:
    - Fam 23: a thin band along strike, FWHM ~550 m x <=125 m (aspect ~4), with 2-3 low-contrast lobes ~200-250 m apart. p_dip 0.05 in both variants; model-free AxB kurtosis -1.8 +/- 0.45 (null -1.1 to 0.5) => flat-topped/bounded along strike. The strongest case for a non-Gaussian patch.
    - Fam 8: two lobes ~230-260 m apart along strike (dip 0.5, p 0.05, bootstrap two-peak rate 0.88 vs null 0.25-0.30). Ambiguous: two sub-patches or a flat-topped elongated patch.
    - Fams 18, 20: flat-topped and elongated along strike. Fams 17, 24: significant only in the excl run.
    - Fams 19, 21: diagonal elongation follows the error ellipse (likely under-deconvolved), so not trusted.
    - Population: patches are flatter than Gaussian (p_dip <= 0.05 in 5/22 incl and 7/21 excl; binomial p 0.004 and <1e-4). The fully model-free A_emp does not confirm this (low power).
    - No ring and no clear multi-asperity structure (dip > 0.6) is robust in both variants.
  - Time changes (each period deconvolved with its own kernel; null = 40 random contiguous blocks): pre vs post M7: p <= 0.05 in 1/22 (incl) and 2/17 (excl) => chance level. Early vs late: 2/22 and 2/21 => chance level. Raw shape differences early/late (10/22) track network improvement (formal sd falls 10-35%), not the source. Centroid shifts |z| > 2 in ~3/44 tests (chance); largest: fam 8, -17 m along strike after the M7 (z -2.7). Down-dip AxB spread of fams 13 and 24 is larger after the M7 (p 0.005-0.015): tentative, possibly the network change. Watch item: fam 23 is marginal for both splits (p 0.05-0.07) but keeps its band shape.
  - Caveats: assumes the full-solution error has the same normalised shape as D (untestable). Common errors (velocity model, overlap pull) count as truth. The null has no burst clustering. p floor 0.05 (20 simulations). Fams 4, 9, 11 skipped; fam 2 incl only.
  - Interpretation: LFE patches are elongated along strike and flat-topped or bounded (uniform slip-zone-like), not Gaussian, and show no resolvable sub-asperities above ~150-200 m. Patch shape does not change at the M7 or over 2008-2026 beyond chance.
- 2026-11-23 ~12:00 SPOTL OCEAN LOADING AT DEPTH + TIDAL REFIT + POROELASTIC TEST. This SUPERSEDES the earlier "ocean load makes slip sense and mu' undecidable" note (2026-11-23 ~03:00).
  - Scripts: scripts/sci_tidal_ocean_spotl.py (stages tests, stress, strainobs, validate, stress2, farcorr, fe, stress4, fit, fit2, poro, dipscan, post, figs), sci_tidal_spotl_lib.py, sci_tidal_spotl_fe.py, sci_tidal_spotl_ocpts.f.
  - Outputs: data/sci/tidal_spotl/ (stress_phasors_final.pkl, convergence_final.csv, fe_validation.csv, validation_*.csv, network_config_fits.csv, per_constituent_*.csv, ocean_scale_profile.csv, family_fits.csv, fit_summary.json, poro_surface_network.csv, poro_joint_tide_m7.csv, poro_summary.json, dip_rake_scan.csv, post_summary.json). Figures: figures/sci_tidal_spotl_{validation,stress,fits,families,poro,dip_rake}.png.
  - SPOTL 3.3.0.2 is installed in tools/spotl (user space, gfortran 8.5; notes in tools/spotl/LFE_INSTALL.txt). Examples ex1-6 pass.
  1. Validation: NOAA gauges (11): usawest (1/30 deg) + TPXO7.2 is best (M2 RMS misfit 1.8 cm, 1.0 cm at the 3 nearest gauges); global models 4-6 cm. PBO strainmeters: adding the SPOTL load cuts the calibration residual from 0.36 to 0.21 (B045 0.19->0.04, B934 0.48->0.17, B935 0.23->0.17, B933 0.55->0.47). All ocean models are indistinguishable. B045 areal strain is anti-phased under unit coupling, and gauge 2 is dead.
  2. Stress at depth: the earlier homogeneous half-space never converged; the layered Earth is 2-5x stiffer at 50-500 km. Final model: layered FE point load (Furlong 1-D) within 2 deg, SPOTL/Farrell plus a depth correction beyond. Converged for RL >=1.5 deg (sigma_n and mean within 2-4%, tau within ~10%). Local modulus 54.6 GPa at 29 km. M2 at the network centroid (kPa), ocean / body / total: tau(RL) 0.076 / 0.29 / 0.216; sigma_n 0.235 / 0.78 / 0.548; mean 0.60 / 1.02 / 0.66. The ocean load is roughly opposite in phase to the body tide.
  3. LFE-rate refit (43,772 units): RIGHT-LATERAL slip holds decisively: dlogL RL 2015 vs LL 27; bootstrap 0/48 prefer LL; robust to all 9 ocean models and all layering variants.
     - mu' = 0.092 +/- 0.013 (bootstrap 0.070-0.116). a*sigma = 0.63 kPa (0.58-0.71) with the local rigidity; the earlier 0.46 corresponds to mu = 30 GPa, so a*sigma scales with the assumed rigidity.
     - LFEs lead total tau by 13 +/- 2.5 deg (3 deg with body tide only). Best strike 280-290. Day-shift null z = 19.8; phase-scramble p = 0.004.
     - UNEXPECTED: body tide alone fits better than body + ocean (dlogL 2139 vs 2017; ocean-scale profile best at 0; 0/48 bootstraps prefer the ocean; ~9-23 after overdispersion phi ~7.9). Either the ocean load at depth is overestimated, the body tide is underestimated, or this is an LFE property. Must be addressed in the paper.
     - Per constituent: a*sigma M2 0.72, N2 0.57, K1 0.22, O1 0.23 kPa. The diurnals need ~3x more sensitivity than M2 (~2x with body only). mu': M2 0.10, N2 0.28, K1 -0.06, O1 -0.09. S2 is not identifiable (absorbed by the time-of-day term).
     - Per family (26): all RL and all positive shear sensitivity. Median a*sigma 0.71 kPa (IQR 0.60-1.32), median mu' 0.25. The westward gradient in lag and mu' remains (lag vs lon rho -0.59, p 0.002; mu' vs lon rho -0.68). 11/26 fit better with body tide only.
  4. POROELASTIC TEST (Shelly & Materna form: CFS = tau + mu(sigma_n - B*mean), tension positive; sign checks pass; dose-response reproduced):
     - Tides: best B = 0, mu = 0.1. A ridge runs over B 0-0.5, mu 0.1-0.25. B = 1, mu = 0.6 (S&M) gives a rate ANTI-correlated with CFS and is rejected (dlogL 2015, ~254 after phi). Rejected in 24/26 families, and also with body tide only (best B 0.3, mu 0.2).
     - M7: CFS < 0 needs mu >=0.48 at B = 1 (0.29-0.51 across families), 0.62 at B 0.7, 0.76 at B 0.5, 1.78 at B 0.
     - JOINT: NO single static (B, mu) explains both the tides and the M7 cessation. The best tidal point within the M7-allowed region (B 0.6, mu 0.7) is dlogL -569 (-72 after phi) from the tidal best and predicts only a 0.83 rate ratio vs the observed 0.45. The tidal-best parameters predict +2.0 kPa dCFS at the M7, i.e. a ~25x rate INCREASE.
     - Interpretation: tidal sensitivity implies weak coupling to normal/mean stress (mu ~0.1, B small), agreeing with Lu, Ide & Ren's tidal mu ~0.1. The M7 cessation needs strong poroelastic clamping (S&M). => The M7 silencing is NOT explained by the same static undrained poroelastic Coulomb response that governs the tides. A non-static or timescale-dependent mechanism is needed: e.g. drained vs undrained behaviour at different periods, permeability or fluid changes (Lu et al.), a nonlinear/threshold response to large steps vs small oscillations, or a dynamic-stress effect. KEY PAPER RESULT.
  5. Shelly et al. dip x rake test (their slope metric, strike 293): body only and converged total both give the maximum at dip 35, rake 180 (RL), with 40/180 close. The LL peak is negligible (0.05 vs 1.55 /kPa). A secondary LL peak appears only when the ocean load is too strong relative to the body tide (e.g. homogeneous half-space loading to 5 deg); that is likely the origin of their LL peak, unconfirmed without their supplement. The ML metric prefers a steeper fault (dip 55, rake 190-200; +377, ~48 after phi), at the grid edge. The slope metric is biased toward low-shear-amplitude orientations. Possibly consistent with Lu et al.'s steep moment tensor (76 deg) (coordinator's query, unverified).
  6. Caveats: 1-D layering (no 3-D slab); plane-stress body tide constant with depth; far-field sigma_zz/sigma_xz neglected; undrained instantaneous poroelasticity assumed; logL ~8x inflated (use bootstrap ranges); strainmeter calibration uncertain.

  - LFE depth minus McCrory interface depth: ours median -0.4 km (59% above the interface); ours with the ~2.5 km depth-bias correction median -2.9 km (96% above); Shelly locations median -2.3 km (100% above, range -8.3 to -1.6). Versus Slab2: Shelly median -0.6 km (100% above).
  - => The LFEs lie just ABOVE the modelled plate interface (~0.5-3 km), i.e. in the overriding plate / accreted material at the slab's southern edge, not on the megathrust. Consistent with Shelly et al. 2026 (Pioneer Fragment, strike-slip edge fault) and Lu & Ide (edge/transform rather than megathrust). Caveat: slab models are poorly constrained at the edge, and our absolute depths have a ~2.5 km bias.

  - Method: within-family comparisons on the same channel and instrument epoch. Each event's S window is matched-filter projected onto the channel stack in 6 bands (1.25-13 Hz); the usable band is 2-6.5 Hz. Indices (log10): LO (2-3 Hz, size), MID ((3-4.5) - (2-3)), HFI ((4.5-6.5) - (2-3)), dD (RMS width of the 2-8 Hz S envelope; typical 0.645 s). Brune sensitivity: dHFI 0.31 and dMID 0.16 per unit ln(fc).
  - Controls: clean events only (no other event in -25..+20 s); metadata and noise change-point epochs; amplitude matching; PB-borehole vs surface split (a source change must appear in both); noise-window control; 200 placebo dates (2009-2021); even/odd halves.
  - Results (27 families pooled, amplitude-matched, z vs placebo): M7 step (+/-180 d): MID +0.014 (z 0.7), HFI -0.020 (z -0.9), LO +0.004 (z 0.5), dD +0.024 s (z 1.7). M7 restart (14/30/60 d): all |z| < 1 (dD z <=1.5). Petrolia: |z| < 0.6. Ferndale: |z| <=1.7. 7/142 tests have |z| > 2 (chance level).
  - Bounds (2 sigma): corner frequency within ~+/-18-25% at the quakes => stress drop at fixed moment changed by less than ~1.7-2x, amplitude by less than ~7%. Restart bounds are weaker (only 160-190 clean events; would detect a corner change >~1.6x; envelope width resolved to +/-0.035 s).
  - Fail the station-group test (path/station effects): M7 envelope broadening +0.037 s (z 2.5) on PB only, gone after 60-240 d; Petrolia surface-only HFI +0.15 (z 3.1).
  - Restart events: without amplitude matching, early post-quake events are larger (~+0.14 log10 in the first 14 d after the M7, and after Ferndale too). Noise also rose (z 2.4-2.8) => a detection-threshold selection effect. Amplitude-matched restart events are spectrally ordinary.
  - 18 yr: no source change common to both station groups (post- vs pre-Petrolia MID -0.02 PB vs +0.018 surface; HFI and dD |z| < 1.2). A surface-only HFI dip in 2016.5-2019.5 is a station artefact.
  - Bursts vs isolated (amplitude- and year-matched, permutation null): in-burst S envelopes are narrower by 8 ms (-1.2%, z -4.0; z -3.3 in each group). TENTATIVE: the null does not cover context such as noise or activity. No spectral difference.
  - Moment-duration scaling (size sorted on one station group, measured on the other; amplitude range 2.5-3.4x): duration barely grows (+0.03-0.06 s per decade, 2-4%). The spectral-slope sign flips with the band used to define size => beta (fc ~ M0^-beta) only bracketed, -0.9 to +0.5. Near-constant duration is favoured; self-similar (1/3) is not excluded.
  - NEW: individual LFEs differ in spectral shape by up to ~+/-0.2 log10, reproduced on independent station sets => real event-to-event source (or within-patch location) variability.
  - INTERPRETATION: the M7 changed WHEN the patches slip, not HOW they rupture. No change in source spectra, duration or size at any quake, at restart, or over 18 yr.
  - Caveats: absolute fc not resolved (2-6.5 Hz band; Brune assumed for stress-drop conversion); envelope widths are path-dominated; small restart samples; the 18-yr null uses 27 split points.
  - Disk: /home is ~92% full; raw/ (3.3 GB) can be deleted.
