  - Method: recovered the absolute per-family noise normalisation of the raw power (it differed 0.1-0.19 log10 between families). S-clean events (no other event in -7..+12 s); weights A^2/(A^4+4) give a typical-event spectrum. Per-station, per-year-matched ratios between families; joint robust LSQ with a per-family value plus a travel-time path term; event and station bootstrap. Indices: LO (2-3.1 Hz size), HFI ((4.9-6.1) - LO falloff), slope, and Brune d ln fc.
  - STRUCTURE: pairs < 3 km form clusters: central (1, 2, 4, 5, 6, 7, 9, 10, 12, 13, 15, 16, 17, 18, 20, 22, 24, 25), south (3, 11, 14), west (8, 19, 26), east (23, 27); 21 isolated. Path-cancelled values are relative within clusters.
  1. Families differ a little: HFI chi2 41.8/22 (p 0.007); intrinsic sd 0.025 log10, i.e. ~8% in fc (range ~+/-25-35%); mostly driven by the west cluster (central alone not significant). LO intrinsic sd 0.044, i.e. ~11% in amplitude. Validation: borehole vs surface HFI consistent (r 0.47, chi2 p 0.96); even/odd r 0.54; vertical/horizontal r 0.58; robust to distance cut, path term and locations. LO shows a borehole/surface inconsistency (some path/radiation contamination). Effective Q ~100 from the path term.
  2. Scaling, within clusters, permutation nulls:
     - SHORTER RECURRENCE -> higher corner: rho -0.51 (p 0.022), Brune rho -0.60 (p 0.006); same sign in 13/13 variants, p < 0.05 in 7/13; not a count/SNR artefact. ~16 covariates tested, so SUGGESTIVE only.
     - Patch size: HFI vs deconvolved size rho -0.40 (p 0.10, n 18); slope consistent with fc ~ 1/L after noise correction, but zero not excluded; sign negative in 12/13 variants.
     - Typical amplitude (LO) vs patch size: NO relation (slope +0.06, 95% -0.36..+0.56). Rejects whole-patch rupture at constant stress drop (+2.2) and constant slip (+1.2).
     - Depth, along-strike position, station distance, slip per event: not significant within clusters. In the global solution LO vs depth rho 0.73 is a cluster-level path effect.
  3. Interpretation: the size range between families is too narrow (+/-11%) to separate self-similar from constant-duration scaling (consistent with the within-family bracket beta -0.9..+0.5). Typical LFE moment is NOT set by patch size; duration possibly is (Brune corner for a ~280 m radius = 2.8-4.9 Hz, in band). So stress drop or slip varies with the patch rather than whole-patch constant-stress-drop rupture. Tentative: recurrence/loading rate may control LFE dynamics.
  - Caveats: absolute fc not resolved; per-family differences only 1-2.5 sigma; values relative within clusters; the recurrence link fails strict multiple-testing correction.
- 2026-11-23 ~17:00 DEPTH EXTENT OF THE DV/V DECLINE (scripts/sci_dvv_depth2.py: stages depthreg, noise, noisefit, lfefit, tables, kernels, invert, diagnose, figs; sci_dvv_depth2_measure.py, sci_dvv_depth2_kernels.py; outputs data/sci/dvv_depth2/ (191 MB; start with summary.json); figures figures/sci_dvv_depth2_{sensor_depth,freq_lapse,kernels,posterior,variants}.png).
  - ANSWER: best supported = top few hundred metres; ~km or deeper is disfavoured but not robustly excluded for the decline. Decline z50 ~0.1-0.3 km, z90 ~0.2-0.7 km; M7 step shallower (z50 ~50-150 m). The constraint comes mainly from KMPB ambient noise; without it the depth is unconstrained.
  1. PB sensor depths (IRIS metadata): B933 139 m, B935 152, B047 175, B046 188, B045 218, B932 412, B934 492, B049 592. B933 and B935 are unusable after cleaning. Decline: surface mean -0.113% (6 sites) vs borehole -0.058% (6); difference 0.051 +/- 0.039 (z 1.3). NO depth trend within the boreholes 175-592 m (+0.02 +/- 0.12 %/km): B932 (412 m) and B934 (492 m) decline ~-0.07%, like the shallow sensors. M7: surface -0.050 vs borehole -0.026. Stations 5-11 km apart differ by 2.6-3.5 sigma, so the change varies over <=10 km (argues against a smooth deep regional source).
  2. LFE coda sub-bands (6 families; V2 cleaning; 29 placebos per channel): surface decline is concentrated in the EARLY coda (2-4 Hz early -0.123 vs late -0.039, z -3.8); boreholes show no lapse dependence. No significant frequency dependence. M7 surface 2-4 Hz early -0.086 vs 4-8 Hz +0.023 (z -2.1).
  3. Noise sub-bands (1-2 / 2-4 / 4-8 Hz): KMPB -0.29 / -0.34 / -0.42; BRIC -0.06 / -0.26 / -0.17; B045 -0.26 / -0.52 / -0.19; HAYF ~0. Early coda > late coda at all three (z 3-11).
  4. Injection tests: paired LFE-coda injections are recovered at 0.996-1.001; the old unpaired fits gave 0.10-0.72. Noise: linear interpolation on the 20 Hz grid under-applies the stretch at 4-8 Hz by up to ~50%; band-limited injection recovers 0.96-1.0.
  5. Inversion: own thin-layer Rayleigh/Love solver (validated), Furlong 2024 model plus shallow LVZ variants, body-wave kernels, buried-receiver treatment, nuisance surface-wave fractions.
     - Decline: uniform H 0.25 km (0.16-0.37), z50 0.13, z90 0.23; exponential D 0.28 km (0.12-0.70), z50 0.19, z90 0.65 (0.28-1.6); two-layer bimodal, P(z50 > 1 km) = 0.24.
     - Velocity-model sensitivity: thin LVZ deeper (uniform z50 0.20; exponential z90 1.1); no LVZ broad.
     - M7: uniform z50 0.065 km, z90 0.12. KMPB noise best fit H ~0.2-0.5 km.
     - The cross-station borehole/surface block alone leans deep (P(z50 > 1 km) ~0.8), but this flips shallow if boreholes are allowed to see near-surface reverberations.
  - Interpretation: a near-receiver change in the top few hundred metres of weathered or fractured rock, deeper than a soil layer of tens of metres. Consistent with non-healing shaking damage plus possible near-surface fluid changes. A km-scale regional process is not required.
  - Caveats: relies mainly on one station's noise; B045's 2-4 Hz peak cannot be fit by fundamental modes (higher modes or reverberation?); large misfits (errors inflated 2-29x); 1-D, fundamental-mode-only kernels; only 6 families in the sub-band LFE analysis.
  - OPEN TENSION: boreholes at 412-592 m decline as much as the 175-218 m sensors, which by itself suggests the change extends below ~0.5 km. This conflicts with the shallow inversion, unless borehole sensors see the near-surface change through surface-reflected or reverberated waves.
- 2026-11-23 JOINT SOURCE-RECEIVER SEPARATION OF DV/V (scripts/sci_dvv_source_receiver.py + sci_dvv_source_receiver_analyze.py; outputs data/sci/dvv_source_receiver/, summary.json; figures figures/sci_dvv_source_receiver_{curves,family,nulls,common,synth,coherence}.png).
  - Method: model d = R_C(t) + S_F(t) + per family x channel offsets, fitted to 1.83 M instrument-cleaned V2 rows (EXCL windows dropped, free offsets at BREAKS2). Gauge: sum_F S_F = 0 (hard constraint); only family deviations dS_F are identifiable, the common deep term is not. Shared node grid: 61 d, 15 d for 120 d after each quake, breaks at Petrolia/Ferndale/M7. lambda = 300 (sensitivity at 100/1000: RMS change 0.008-0.013% vs bootstrap SD 0.037%). Sparse exact LSQ with CC weights + Huber.
  - Primary panel (balanced): 8 long-record stations (PB B045, B046, B047, B049, B932, B934, KMR, KMPB), 26 channel-segments, 25 families. The 2019+ BK stations are a sensitivity test.
  - Errors: honest SE = max(bootstrap, placebo, label permutation). Bootstrap is calibrated (split-half ratio 0.9-1.1). Placebos understate the noise at the M7 by ~2x. Cochran Q/dof ~0.4-0.9, so the SE is somewhat conservative.
  - Limitation: changes during the silence are not separable from the coseismic step, because there are no LFEs to measure. The model reports a step, a 60-d transient, and their total.
  1. Family-specific source terms are null (Q, 24 dof; Q / p / tau95): M7 step 12.7 / 0.97 / 0.030%; 60-d transient 13.1 / 0.96 / 0.13%; total 9.5 / 0.996 / 0.10%; 4-yr decline 15.0 / 0.92 / 0.062%; Ferndale 21.3 / 0.62 / 0.035%; Petrolia 13.0 / 0.97 / 0.020%.
     - No family has |z| > 1.6. Regressions on silence duration (1st/10th event) and on modelled dp: Holm p = 1. Slope bounds: < 0.0027 %/day, < 0.08 %/kPa. The all-channel variant is also null.
     - Lead: family 27, 60-d transient -0.15 +/- 0.06% (z -2.7). Stable under station deletion but not significant after correction for 25 families (Bonferroni p 0.17-0.24).
     - No spatial pattern (along-strike, depth, distance to M7). The along-strike slope has z_boot 3.6 but permutation p = 0.30, driven by family 27.
  2. The common deep term is bounded only (apparent w_S*S_bar): M7 90-d: common mode -0.039 +/- 0.029% (95% [-0.095, +0.018]); B047 -0.005 +/- 0.020; HAYF +0.018 +/- 0.034. Damage-only bound: a deep drop larger than 0.044% is excluded. Decline: common mode -0.067 +/- 0.028% (95% [-0.12, -0.01]), equally consistent with near-surface decline at every station. Smallest station B046 -0.033 +/- 0.026. Damage-only bound >= -0.074%.
  3. Stress sensitivity: apparent bounds |M7| <~0.03-0.09%, decline <~0.08-0.12%.
     - Local (within 10 km, w_S 0.05-0.12): M7 <=0.25-1.9%, i.e. <=1.1-8 per MPa for |dp| = 2.3 kPa. Deep distributed (w_S 0.35-0.8): <=0.04-0.27%, i.e. <=0.16-1.2 per MPa.
     - Expected for the LFE zone: 0.01-0.1 per MPa (lab 1e-4-3e-2 per MPa; SAFOD 0.24 per MPa at 1 km), i.e. 10-1000x below our bounds.
     - Conclusion: this rules out percent-level local changes (MPa-scale pore-pressure transients or damage) but not kPa-level static stressing.
  4. Synthetics (real design): leakage floor 0.015% (step), 0.06% (transient), 0.03% (decline). Injected family steps recovered with slope 1.10; other-family leakage 0.019% RMS. A common -0.03% step does not leak into dS_F. Synthetic noise is only 0.5-0.7x the real noise. Local bounds scale as w_true/w_assumed; the factor-of-10 uncertainty in w_S dominates.
  5. w_S is not measurable: inter-family coda coherence is at chance (gamma ~0.1), and only 0.2-0.3 even for direct S.
  - Answer to the user's question "can't we see deep changes": the shared deep path is not identifiable from coda stretching alone. Family deviations are null at the ~0.03-0.1% level. Deep changes are bounded but not detected.
  - Caveat: coda windows end at the noise level (fixed windows would be cleaner; not redone).
- 2026-11-23 DEEP VS SHALLOW DV/V SEPARATION [SUPERSEDED/WITHDRAWN (see 2026-11-25 code review)] (scripts/sci_dvv_deep_shallow{_measure,,_repdl,_rep,_summary,_figs}.py; outputs data/sci/dvv_deep_shallow/: summary.json, deep_estimates.csv, station_directS_coda.csv, shallow_from_directS.csv, tension_*.csv, swin_station_terms.csv, clock_*.csv, synthetic_recovery.csv, rep/; figures figures/sci_dvv_deep_shallow_{deep,directS,lapse,clock,repeaters}.png).
  - Honest SE = max(bootstrap, robust placebo). The robust placebo drops 1 of 15 placebos, the 12.25-yr shift whose fake window falls in the 2008-12 instrument-problem era; keeping it raises the decline SE to ~0.034%. EXCL/BREAKS2 applied everywhere.
  1. Direct-S travel-time inversion (main result).
     - Data: stacks of 100 isolated LFEs (50 in 182 d post-mainshock), 27 families x ~70 channels; S and P delays at 200 Hz with bootstrap errors. A per-stack origin term is removed, so only relative path delays are used (cancels origin time and common clock).
     - Deep layer (z >=1 km, uniform), preferred empirical delay/coda-ratio model [SUPERSEDED/WITHDRAWN (see 2026-11-25 code review)] with surface and borehole stations separate: M7 -0.002 +/- 0.009% (95% -0.020...+0.016); decline -0.002 +/- 0.009%. Other models: kernel joint model (h 0.1-1 km) -0.005 to -0.017 +/- 0.009%. Station terms free: M7 -0.04 +/- 0.06, decline -0.08 +/- 0.05 (weak). Deep annual amplitude <=~0.005%.
     - The coda common mode from source-receiver (M7 -0.039 +/- 0.029, decline -0.067 +/- 0.028) is therefore NOT deep. Decline excluded at 7 sigma with the robust error, 1.9 sigma with all placebos.
     - Synthetics: deep-only injection recovered x1.24-1.30 (M7), x1.03 (decline). Realistic shallow injections leak <=0.004% into the preferred estimate.
     - Coda is more near-surface-sensitive than the body-wave kernel: S delay per unit coda dv/v is 1.7-2.1 s vs ~8 s predicted. So the kernel model misfits (chi2r 2.4) and the empirical model is preferred.
  2. Shallow layer, from surface-station S delay relative to the borehole mean. Decline: KMPB +7.5 +/- 1.1 ms, RBOW +5.6, KMR +4.9, HAYF +4.6 => about -4 to -6% in the top 100 m (-2 to -3% over 300 m). M7: RBOW +4.3, SPRL +3.6 ms => -2.5 to -3.5% in the top 100 m. These conversions depend on the velocity model (about 3x larger without a low-velocity zone).
  3. Borehole tension: partly resolved, about 2 sigma. Decline: borehole S delay scales with the borehole coda decline. Onset window [S+/-0.4 s], which excludes the surface ghost: 7.5 +/- 3.2 s per unit. A change entirely above the sensors predicts 0 (chi2r 11); a change just below predicts ~8 (chi2r 2.0). So the borehole decline extends below 0.4-0.6 km but not below 1 km. Supporting: borehole late coda (15-25 s) -0.020 +/- 0.008% vs surface -0.006 +/- 0.010%. Only 6 stations; leverage mainly B045 vs B046/B049. M7: borehole ratio 0.1 +/- 1.9, so the M7 change is above the sensors (consistent with depth2 z50 50-150 m).
  4. Timing check: monthly S wander is 1.7-3 ms RMS. Steps: B933 2021-11 (known); KMPB 2016-02 +4.6 ms (new, unexplained); PRDS 2024-06 -3.8 ms (BK reinstall period); B049 2024-12 -3.3 ms (z -3.2); BRIC 2022-11 (in its excluded window). KMR S-P decline +4.2 +/- 2.5 ms (not a clock drift, 1.7 sigma). The first post-M7 month has a ~15 ms PB/NC vs BK offset, so the direct-S 60-d transient is not interpretable.
  5. Ordinary repeating earthquakes: NCSN M >=1.5 2015-2026, NCEDC waveforms at 9 stations. 353 clusters at CC >=0.95: 218 deep (median 19 km), 73 shallow (median 8 km). Coda S+3-20 s, 2-8 Hz, same time model.
     - Network means: deep M7 -0.043 +/- 0.010%, shallow -0.066 +/- 0.008%, difference +0.020 +/- 0.013. Decline: deep -0.025 +/- 0.008, shallow -0.028 +/- 0.006, difference +0.006 +/- 0.008. This independently confirms the M7 drop and the decline. No source-depth dependence in the network mean, so the change is receiver-side.
     - LEAD (coordinator's check of rep/rep_fit.csv): at the two largest-decline stations, deep repeaters show about twice the decline of shallow ones. KMPB -0.112 +/- 0.027 vs -0.054 +/- 0.013 (diff 1.9 sigma); B045 -0.103 +/- 0.021 vs -0.051 +/- 0.011 (2.2 sigma). Others go the other way (B046, B047), and the network mean difference is ~0. Not significant over 9 stations. Could be path or azimuth differences between the cluster sets.
     - 2026-11-25 LEAD CLOSED: per-station deep-minus-shallow decline differences (from data/sci/dvv_deep_shallow/rep/rep_fit.csv, bootstrap SE): KMPB -0.058+/-0.030 (z -1.9), B045 -0.052+/-0.024 (-2.2), B932 +0.034+/-0.012 (+2.9, opposite sign), B046 +1.0, HAYF +0.7, B047 +0.6, KMR -0.7, BRIC -0.5, B049 0.0. Inverse-variance mean +0.006 +/- 0.006% (null). chi2 19.3/9 dof (p 0.02) = heterogeneity with both signs => station/path scatter or understated bootstrap SE (x1.5 SE gives p ~0.5), not a systematic deep decline. M7 deep-minus-shallow: mean +0.020 +/- 0.011%, chi2 14.0/9 (p 0.12), null. Conclusion: no source-depth dependence of the decline or M7 drop; changes are receiver-side/shallow.
     - Errors are cluster-bootstrap only (too few pre-2021 pairs for a placebo). Offshore catalog depths are uncertain.
  6. Fixed lapse windows: decline, surface: 3-8 s -0.100 +/- 0.015%, 8-15 s +0.005, 15-25 s -0.006. Boreholes: -0.053 / -0.024 / -0.020. M7, surface: -0.121 / -0.035 / 0.000. Boreholes: -0.04 to -0.05 at all lapse times. The joint shift+stretch fit was too noisy to use.
  - CONCLUSION: the deep crust (>=1 km down to the LFEs) did not change at the M7 or over the decline [SUPERSEDED/WITHDRAWN (see 2026-11-25 code review)], to about +/-0.02% (95%). The changes are in the upper crust at each receiver: the M7 change in the top ~100 m, the decline in the top hundreds of metres, reaching about 0.4-0.8 km at boreholes (2 sigma).
  - The intermediate files rep/wf_raw.npy and rep/wf_1_12.npy (6.3 GB) were deleted; rerunning the load stage regenerates them.
- 2026-11-24 SOURCE-REGION DV/V FROM S-P TIMES (scripts/sci_dvv_source_region{_measure,,_rep,_comp,_figs,_sp}.py; outputs data/sci/dvv_source_region/: summary.json, timefit_network.csv, timefit_reccorr.csv, family_terms_*.csv, synthetic_*.csv, placebo_repeaters_as_sources.csv, comp/, rep/, sp_scan.csv; figures figures/sci_dvv_source_region_{estimates,timeseries,synth,family,spscan}.png).
  - Why S-P: S-P is origin-time- and clock-free. A change in a volume around all sources is otherwise absorbed by the origin term, and it trades off with centroid depth (dz ~ -2*H*eps). This follows Fable's design.
  - Method: 555 epoch stacks, 27 families, ~400 isolated LFEs each. Breaks at Petrolia, Ferndale and the M7, and again 60 d after each. The 0-60 d window is one stack per family with 5-230 events. P and S delays measured per channel; median bootstrap SE: P 9 ms, S 4 ms. Joint fit per family and stack: dt0, dx, dy, dz and eps, with 3D travel-time gradients and kernels. A network time model runs across families. Honest SE = max(bootstrap, placebo); placebos are date shifts of 5.25-12.25 yr, 1.5-2x the bootstrap.
  - Results, 10-km sphere / 5-km sphere, receiver-corrected with the deep_shallow station S terms: M7 step -0.07 +/- 0.14% / -0.16 +/- 0.28; Restart 0-60 d -0.15 +/- 0.11 / -0.29 +/- 0.21; Ferndale -0.06 +/- 0.12 / -0.15 +/- 0.23; Decline +0.04 +/- 0.07 / +0.08 +/- 0.15.
  - 95% bounds (10-km): M7 -0.36 to +0.21; restart -0.37 to +0.07; Ferndale -0.30 to +0.17; decline -0.10 to +0.19. This excludes damage or fluid effects of >=0.3-0.4% in 10 km (>=0.6-0.7% in 5 km), 3-4 orders of magnitude above the poroelastic expectation.
  - Slab model (5 km), which separates S and P (uncorrected): M7: eps_S -0.06 +/- 0.07, eps_P 0.00 +/- 0.19, d ln(Vp/Vs) +0.06 +/- 0.14. Restart: -0.08 / +0.07 / +0.12 +/- 0.12. Ferndale: +0.04 / +0.03 / 0.00. Decline: -0.08 / -0.14 / -0.07 +/- 0.10. So there is no Vp/Vs change: no evidence of the fluid dilatancy/drainage proposed by Lu, Ide & Ren, within ~+/-0.2-0.3% (95%).
  - Per-family terms are homogeneous (Cochran Q p >=0.97).
  - The small negative restart value is near-receiver and compositional: surface stations -0.66 +/- 0.32 vs boreholes -0.01 +/- 0.51 (5-km). Composition-matched stacks roughly halve it: -0.13 +/- 0.07 matched vs -0.23 all.
  - Centroid shifts (corrected, 5-km): M7 dz +0.7 +/- 5.5 m, restart -5.9 +/- 5.8 m. S-only fingerprint: restart dx/dy/dz all < +/-5 m. The raw Ferndale dz of +15 m and the boreholes-only decline dz of -28 m are receiver S-pattern aliasing. So the restart LFEs are at the same place to within ~5-10 m.
  - Synthetics: waveform-level 5-km sphere -0.2% recovered -0.197 to -0.206 (ratio 1.00). Location shifts dz +10/+20 m and dx +30 m leak 0.000 once location is solved (0.128%/10 m if dz is fixed). A slab change seen by the sphere model has kernel ratio 2.6. A restart-only change stays in the restart term.
  - Placebos with repeaters as sources are weak, because single events are 4-60x noisier: 19-km class +0.02 +/- 0.64 corrected; decline +1.25 +/- 0.56 bootstrap only, ~2 sigma.
  - The 19-km repeater receiver correction is too noisy (2-6 ms per station; 15-31 clusters span the M7) and was not used for the primary. The systematic leakage from the receiver correction is -0.04 to -0.05% (10-km).
  - Splitting is not recomputed: the existing SWS bound is ~1-2 ms over the whole path. Source-side S-to-P conversion scan: nothing robust (inconsistent interface heights, peak at the edge of the search). Repeaters deeper than 30 km: none span the M7.
  - Caveats: the sphere design cannot separate eps_S from eps_P, and the slab eps_P is weak. The common-mode receiver S-P change is unconstrained, so the correction is model-dependent. In the 3D model, Vs = Vp/1.78 and take-off angles are shallow (cos i ~0.3).
  - OVERALL CONCLUSION, combining all deep tests: no deep change anywhere. 1 km to the LFEs along the paths: within +/-0.02% (direct S). Within 10 km of the LFEs: within ~+/-0.3% (95%; S-P), for the M7, the restart, Ferndale and the decline. Vp/Vs is unchanged, and restart locations are unchanged to within ~5-10 m. All observed velocity changes are in the top ~0.1-0.8 km under the stations.
- 2026-11-24 A) BK.JEWT AMBIENT-NOISE DV/V, full record 2023-08-23 to 2026-11-23. Station: JEWT sits above the LFE families, 2-8 km horizontally. Script: scripts/sci_noise_dvv_jewt.py (stages local, download, m7local, dvv, power, compare, plot); imports sci_noise_dvv.py unchanged. Outputs: data/sci/noise_dvv_jewt/ (jewt_noise_steps.csv, series, recovery windows, longterm fits, lapse steps, power regression, correlations, m7_62d_* interim). Figures: figures/sci_noise_dvv_jewt_{timeseries,m7,steps,compare,power,m7_62d}.png.
  - Data: 1189 days, only 2 missing. STS-5A, flat 0.05-10 Hz. StationXML epoch break at 2024-02-28 (identical response), given a free offset. Reference: the pre-M7 mean. 196 placebo dates.
  - Bands: the user's 0.1-1 Hz (24 h, 600-s segments, coda 10-60 s, cross-components only, because the 0.1-1 Hz autocorrelation coda is the whitening-taper response), 1-3 Hz (coda 3-30 s) and 3-5 Hz (coda 1-8 s), plus the method bands 1-4 and 2-8 Hz (09-11 UTC).
  - M7 30-day step: autocorrelations (AC) ~0 in every band (1-3 Hz +0.006 +/- 0.030). Cross-components (SC): 1-3 Hz 24 h -0.079 +/- 0.034 (z -2.3); 1-3 Hz 09-11 UTC -0.18 +/- 0.06 (z -3.2, 0/196 placebos as low); 1-4 Hz -0.19 +/- 0.08 (z -2.5); 2-8 Hz SC -0.07 +/- 0.06; 3-5 Hz not resolved; 0.1-1 Hz SC -0.60 +/- 0.24, but NOT velocity: it tracks microseism power (r -0.46), and the annual amplitude falls from 0.13 to 0.03% with power as a regressor. Same calendar date in other years (1-3 Hz SC): 2023 +0.01, 2025 -0.05, so part of the step is the winter low.
  - Lapse dependence: the SC drop is entirely in the early coda (3-10 s: -0.16 to -0.27; 2-8 s: -0.29); the late coda shows nothing. So it is shallow or near-station, or a wavefield change.
  - Recovery (SC, 09-11 1-3 Hz): days 1-10 -0.28 +/- 0.11, days 11-25 -0.20, days 26-60 -0.22, days 61-120 -0.09, days 241-365 ~+0.02. Velocity was at its low during the LFE silence, with no recovery inside the silence; recovery took 2-4 months (fitted tau ~20 d, permanent part -0.07 to -0.10% at 1-3/1-4 Hz SC). AC stays ~0.
  - Other events (M4.8 2023-10, M5.25 2024-12-15, M5.1/M5.6 2026-06): no consistent step.
  - Annual cycle: 1-4 Hz 0.128 +/- 0.014%, peak ~Jul 19 (dry-season maximum, like BRIC); 1-3 Hz 0.037%.
  - Trend: not determined (3.25 years can't separate a permanent M7 offset from a trend). No sign of the post-2021 decline seen at KMPB, BRIC and B045.
  - Comparisons: matches LFE coda at JEWT (HHE -0.10, HHN/HHZ ~0); monthly r with HHE 0.75. At the M7, JEWT is like KMPB and far below BRIC (-0.29/-0.64) and B045 (-0.34/-0.75). SC correlates with the other stations' 2-8 Hz SC (r 0.6-0.8), a regional component (wavefield or hydrology).
  - Caveat: the SC/AC difference could be noise directionality (storms, aftershocks) rather than velocity.
- 2026-11-24 B) JEWT 2-day moving stacks, 62 days around the M7. Script: scripts/sci_noise_dvv_jewt_2day.py (made in the main session). Output: data/sci/noise_dvv_jewt/m7_2day_moving.csv. Figures: figures/sci_noise_dvv_jewt_m7_2day.png (all/AC/SC) and _2day_sc.png (cross-components only, per pair).
  - 1-3 Hz: AC/ALL flat within +/-0.04%; SC drifts to -0.15 to -0.2% during the silence and stays there into January.
  - 3-5 Hz SC: erratic (-1% in days 1-4, +1.5% on Dec 10-11); pre-M7 swings already +/-0.9%. 0.1-1 Hz: swings +/-1%.
- 2026-11-24 C) Multi-station single-station cross-component noise dv/v in the user's bands around the three quakes. Script: scripts/sci_noise_dvv_bands_quakes.py (main session; stages correlate, dvv, plot).
  - Data: data on disk (-35 to +30 d), ~22 stations per quake, whole-day one-bit correlations; bands and coda windows 0.1-1 Hz 8-50 s, 1-3 Hz 3-25 s, 3-5 Hz 2-15 s. Only EN/EZ/NZ pairs with median CC >=0.3 are used. Step = post days 1-15 minus pre days -30 to -1.
  - Outputs: data/sci/noise_dvv_bands_quakes/ (ccf_*.npz, daily_pairs.csv, daily.csv, steps.csv). Figure: figures/sci_noise_dvv_bands_quakes.png.
  - Median steps (Petrolia / Ferndale / M7): 0.1-1 Hz: +0.13 / +0.04 / +0.03 (no drop); 1-3 Hz: -0.11 / -0.15 / -0.15 (9 of 14-22 stations below -2 sigma); 3-5 Hz: -0.10 / -0.15 / -0.10. Largest M7 1-3 Hz drops: BLAS -0.6, B046/B047 -0.39, RBOW -0.38.
  - The drop appears only above 1 Hz, consistent with a shallow change.
  - CAVEAT: all three quakes are in December (winter-rain onset), so part of each step may be seasonal; ~2-month windows can't separate the two. The same-date-other-years check at JEWT suggests a -0.05% winter part.
  - Errors: 5-d block bootstrap and in-window placebos (limited).
- 2026-11-24 JEWT cross-component-only full-record view (scripts/sci_noise_dvv_jewt_sc_full.py -> figures/sci_noise_dvv_jewt_sc_full.png and data/sci/noise_dvv_jewt/jewt_sc_full_summary.csv). Re-plots existing JEWT products (no reprocessing) as cross-component-only (EN, EZ, NZ per pair + combined 5-day/10-day/monthly) over the whole JEWT record 2023-08-23..2026-11 (station installed Aug 2023, so Petrolia 2021 and Ferndale 2022 are not covered), bands 0.1-1, 1-3, 3-5 Hz.
  - Results (combined SC): 1-3 Hz M7 step -0.079% (placebo sd 0.034, z -2.3), drop to ~-0.10% held through ~May 2025, recovery to 0 by ~Oct 2025, seasonal amp 0.032%. Note a similar-size dip in Feb-Apr 2024 (winter, near the 2024-02-28 StationXML epoch change), so the M7 drop is only ~1x the largest non-quake excursion.
  - 3-5 Hz: step -0.097% (placebo sd 0.165, z -0.6, not significant), slow drift to -0.3..-0.5% through 2025. 0.1-1 Hz: -0.60% (z -2.5) but microseism-power dominated, not reliable.
- 2026-11-24 The joint noise+direct-S deep fit agent was stopped at the user's request (not wanted).
- 2026-11-24 JEWT 2-day BACKWARD moving stacks (day d = CCFs of d-1 and d; no future leakage), whole record 2023-08-23..2026-11, cross-component only (EN, EZ, NZ, all three pass pair selection in all bands), ref = mean of all pre-M7 days. Script scripts/sci_noise_dvv_jewt_2day_back.py -> data/sci/noise_dvv_jewt/jewt_2day_back.csv, figures/sci_noise_dvv_jewt_2day_back_{full,m7}.png (trailing 30-day median line; a centred mean was rejected because it smears the M7 drop backwards, and a trailing mean was rejected for spikes from bad days at the Feb 2024 gap).
  - Results: 1-3 Hz level ~+0.03% before -> ~-0.07% by Jan 2025 -> 0 by ~Sep 2025, but the decline begins late Nov 2024 (dip ~Nov 22) and first post-M7 days are aftershock-contaminated, so the sharp step (days 1..15 minus -30..-1) is -0.001% (daily scatter 0.059%).

- 2026-11-24 ~23:30 TIME-LAPSE TELESEISMIC RECEIVER FUNCTIONS DONE. Scripts scripts/sci_rf_{catalog,download,compute,static,timelapse,power,model,summary,raysum_prep,model_raysum,rfpy_check}.py, run_sci_rf_all.sh, run_sci_rf_post.sh; waveforms /data/wsd02/maleen_data/MTJ_RF/wf; outputs data/sci/rf_timelapse/ (summary.json, station_table.csv, network_combined.csv, results_all.csv, power_*.csv, power_corr_*.csv, model_raysum.csv, static_picks.csv, qc_all.csv); figures figures/sci_rf_timelapse_overview.png, sci_rf_power_model.png, sci_rf_rfpy_check_KMPB.png, per-station sci_rf_static_*/timelapse_*/power_*. New conda env 'rf' (python 3.10, numpy 1.24) holds PyRaysum 1.0.0 + RfPy 0.1.2 (user-approved; would not build in lfe). Fixed pandas read_json(str) bug in sci_rf_summary.py (io.StringIO).
  - Method: 5,800 USGS M>=5.5 30-95 deg 2008-2026; iterative time-domain RFs a=1,2.5,5,10 + modified Wiener (Audet 2010); QC SNR>=3, fit>=80% (accepted 17-201 RFs per station; 14 stations with enough for time-lapse); matched-geometry (30 deg baz x 2 slowness bins) contrasts: steps at Petrolia/Ferndale/M7, after vs before 2021-12-20, linear trend; quantities direct-P amplitude & polarisation, shallow 0.15-1 s delay/amp, 2.5-4 s delay/amp, stretch; nulls = bootstrap + event-time permutation + placebo; injections 20 ms / 10% / 0.5% stretch recovered (15-24 ms, 10-12%, 0.50-0.54%). Herath & Audet power method: multitaper PSD 1.25-3 Hz of R and T in slab, crust and pre-P noise windows, 1.5-yr windows 95% overlap, baz-reweighted; PyRaysum dipping-layer models from Bloch 2023 grids.
  - Results: phases match slab model (e.g. B047 3.26 vs 3.31 s; B049 slab Moho 4.2 vs 3.8 s). Detection limits (2 sigma): shallow delay ~20-60 ms, amplitude ~6-15% at best stations; expected shallow dv/v signal <1 ms / ~0.2% -> undetectable.
  - 484 station tests: 6.6% |z|>2, 8 with |z|>3 (1.3 expected), but they are artefacts/candidates: B933 long-term drops in ALL windows incl. pre-P noise (-3 to -4 dB) = known instrument break 2021-11-01; RVIT Ferndale shallow delay 150 ms = search-limit value; KMR M7 crust power -1.8 dB (z -3.5) with 25 deg polarisation change = likely sensor/orientation; B049 shallow amp trend +2%/yr (z 4.0) and B932 2.5-4 s amp trend (z 3.4) are isolated candidates.
  - Network combined: M7 polarisation +4.0 deg (z 3.0) and post-2021 stretch +0.54% (z 3.0; trend z 3.6) are not credible: chi2 shows excess scatter and the network combination assumes station independence although stations share the same events (common event-mix/geometry), and a 0.5% stretch would contradict the +/-0.02% deep direct-S bound by 25x.
  - Power method: no robust steps; power-vs-external correlations 46/600 at p<0.05 (7.7% vs 5%), and significant correlations also occur in the pre-P NOISE window (e.g. KMPB noise T vs coda dv/v r=-0.79), so correlations are not diagnostic.
  - PyRaysum: event geometry alone moves slab-window power 0.5-1.7 dB; minimum detectable LVL Vs change (2 sigma, after vs before 2021-12-20) median ~17% (best KMPB 3.3%: dVs -1.1 +/- 1.65%); B047 +13% and B935 -16% (opposite signs, ~2 sigma) consistent with chance; B933 off-scale (instrument break). RfPy check KMPB: stack cc 0.97, constant -4 dB power offset.
  - CONCLUSION: no credible time-lapse change in receiver functions; bounds LVL Vs change to ~+/-3% (KMPB) to tens of % elsewhere; Herath-size changes (Vs tens of % in a 2-km layer) are excluded at the best stations, but much smaller valve-type changes are not.
- 2026-11-25 RESTART POLARITY TEST DONE (scripts/sci_restart_polarity.py; env POST_DELAY_D shifts POST start; outputs data/sci/restart_polarity{,_delay7d}/{per_channel,per_family,placebo_per_family,summary}.csv; figures/sci_restart_polarity{,_delay7d}.png).
  - Method: stored signed template CC ('ccs', S window S-0.5..S+3 s, 2-8 Hz, extremum within +/-3 samples of lag 0) from data/sci/remote_check/perchan. Per family and quake: last 50 events before vs first 20 after; channels with pre mean >=0.10. Flip = post mean < 0 with t <= -2. Null = 300 placebo dates 2009-2024 (>=90 d from the quakes).
  - M7 (post events median 18.6 d after): 11/1740 channels flipped (0.6%; placebo median 0.7%, 44th pct); post/pre 1.00 (73rd pct); pattern r 0.81 (93rd pct); surface 0.96, borehole 0.94. NO polarity or waveform change at restart.
  - Petrolia: 1.1-1.6% flips (83-93rd pct), ratio 0.98 with 7-d delay (51st pct). Null.
  - Ferndale: anomalous even with a 7-d delay (flips 2.2%, 97th pct; ratio 0.85, 1.7th pct; pattern r 0.56, below all placebos). Surface-only: surface post/pre 0.72 (17 flips), PB borehole 1.08 (4 flips). Driven by BRIC, RVIT, RBOW, KMPB, HAYF; monthly ratios show RVIT decorrelated for months (~0.7-0.8) and BRIC degrading to 0.2-0.3 by Apr-Jun 2023 (station problem). Interpretation: near-surface/station decorrelation, NOT a source change (a source change must appear in borehole too).
- 2026-11-25 From the paper re-read: Shelly & Materna 2026 describe a TEMPORARY cessation; the 'permanent' element is the static coseismic stress/strain (dp ~ -2.5 kPa, volumetric strain ~4e-8), predicted dv/v ~0.0004-0.004% (<=0.04% even with shallow-crust sensitivity), 5-50x below our deep bound (-0.002 +/- 0.009%). Not a contradiction with our null.
- 2026-11-25 RF LVZ TOP/BOTTOM TIME-LAPSE DONE (scripts/sci_rf_lvz_synth.py [rf env], scripts/sci_rf_lvz.py [lfe env, --from_pkl]; outputs data/sci/rf_lvz/{stations_lvz,contrasts_lvz,windows_lvz,variability_lvz,sensitivity_lvz,detection_lvz}.csv, summary.json, replicates.pkl, synth_*.npz; figures figures/sci_rf_lvz_{static,timeseries,contrasts,sensitivity}.png).
  - Context: Gosselin et al. 2020-style observable; MTJ LFEs are near-continuous (no ETS cycle) so slip-phase binning does not apply; LFE-activity-state bins were run anyway (null, all within 1.2 sigma) but are not interpreted.
  - Stations: slab Moho (m) positive Ps seen at ~15/26 stations; top-of-LVZ (t) negative Ps mostly absent. Usable pair: PB.B049 (t 2.25 s vs synth 2.15, A -0.044, z 7.8; m 4.23 s vs 3.81; n=139); BK.HAYF weak (n=80, from 2020); BK.RBOW excluded (injection +50 ms recovered as 5 ms). LVL base c not separable (LVL 1.3-2.8 km thick). Multiples only candidates.
  - Physics: LVL Vs change moves t and m together (interference with c), so dT(m-t) is nearly blind (13-30 ms per 0.1 km/s); the sensitive observable is A_t or A_t/A_m (+20-26% per -0.1 km/s).
  - Results (B049+HAYF; shared-event bootstrap/permutation/placebo): post-2021 dT +39+/-37 ms, A_t +21+/-18% (= contrast +0.10+/-0.07 km/s, 1.4 sigma); M7 dT -44+/-82, A_t -20+/-21, ratio +1+/-45 (A_m -23+/-11, Moho conversion, not LVZ); Ferndale dT +114+/-78 (94th pct); Petrolia A_t +135+/-74 (z 3.1) NOT credible (7 vs 7 events, vanishes with +/-0.5 s windows, HAYF sign flips, implies 0.6 km/s); seasonal inconsistent between stations; B049 long-term trends dT +5.7+/-5.6 ms/yr, A_t +0.5+/-3.3 %/yr, chi2 15.1/10 (44th pct); m-t 1.77-2.01 s in all windows. 112 tests: 4 |z|>2, 2 |z|>3 (same Petrolia) = chance.
  - Detection limits (2 sigma): post-2021/seasonal dT 68-77 ms, A_t 30-40% = 0.14-0.16 km/s LVL contrast = 5-6% Vs; quake steps 0.27-0.41 km/s (10-15% Vs). Gosselin 0.05-0.1 km/s is 1.5-3x below the long-term limit; Herath-scale 2-4% Vs undetectable. Injection +50 ms recovered 46/44 ms (B049/HAYF).
  - VERDICT: no credible LVZ change; limits are weak (one good station).

  - Corrected Herath power-method LVL Vs (radial): long-term post-2021 2-sigma detection limit KMPB 4.7% (was 3.3%), B935 5.6, B049 8.4, B932 9.9, B047 11.8; median ~18% (was ~17%). M7 step: best USAL 9.5%, KMPB 10.9%, median ~23%.
  - Estimates with |z|>2 (6 of ~100 station x contrast tests, chance level ~5): B047 post-2021 -14.1+/-5.9% (sign flipped vs pre-fix +13.4), B935 post-2021 -6.7+/-2.8 (pre-fix -16.2), BRIC Petrolia -32.8+/-14.3, KMR Ferndale -27.6+/-12.1 and M7 +40+/-19.5 (KMR orientation/sensor issue), B933 post-2021 +54.9+/-10.6 (known instrument break).

- 2026-11-25 LFE-STACK SECONDARY PHASES + ATTENUATION THROUGH TIME DONE (scripts/sci_lfe_phases_{common,extract,atten,timelapse,summary,figs}.py, sci_lfe_phases.py; outputs data/sci/lfe_phases/: summary.json, search_robustness.csv, search_injection.csv, search_scan_*.csv, predicted_delays.csv, geometry.csv, atten_tests.csv, atten_yearly.csv, atten_station_placebo_z.csv, atten_qpqs.json, timelapse_*.csv; stacks/ 1.5 GB + raw/ 0.6 GB needed only for reruns; figures figures/sci_lfe_phases_{scan,sections,atten,timelapse}.png).
  - Search: isolated LFEs (+/-5 s), 1-15 Hz, 947 family-station pairs; Bloch t/m per family; 1D ray tracing (S-P model/picked median 1.03). Predicted SmS-S 0.27-0.95 s (21 deep families, 1.5-3.3 km above m) and 0.71-2.85 s (6 shallow). Hypotheses SmS, PmS, PmP, SmP (below sources), Sp, Ps (top of layer above). Robust rule: z>=3 under all-family AND within-depth-class depth-shuffle nulls, z>=2 in borehole, surface, odd and even families.
  - RESULT: 0/34 hypotheses robust. Three all-family candidates (SmS MF m-3.2 km z 3.1; Sp t+6 km z 3.2-4.9; SmS envelope m+0.1 km z 3.2) drop to <=2.5 within class and fail subsets: shallow vs deep coda-level difference, not height-scaled moveout (explains the earlier inconsistent S-to-P scan).
  - Detection: injected SmS 0.3x S gives z 4.0-4.1 (all stations); boreholes need 0.35-0.5x. A sharp slab Moho should give 0.5-1x S at 2-8 Hz, so a sharp m at these frequencies is disfavoured (or 3D depth variation scatters it).
  - Time-lapse: no phase, so the predicted-SmS window is receiver-dominated S coda (placebo SD 0.136%): Petrolia -0.07% (z -0.5), Ferndale +0.38 (2.8), M7 +0.25 (1.8), restart +0.38 (2.8); coda, not in-layer. Injection +1% delay on a phase of amplitude 1.0 recovered 39-47% => in-layer dv/v 2-sigma limit +/-0.5-0.6% (amp 1.0) / +/-1.9-2.2% (amp 0.3).
  - Attenuation (P-window spectra, dt*, P/S; 317-347 placebos): all network |z|<1.4. 2-sigma limits: dt*_S +/-10 ms (180 d) / +/-14 ms (60 d); dt*_P +/-21/25; d(t*P-t*S) +/-21/29. Long-term (2022-M7 vs 2015-21): dt*_S -0.2 ms (z -0.5), P-S -8.8 ms (z -1.2). Station tests 4.4% |z|>2 (chance). dt*_S +/-10 ms => path-averaged d(1/Qs) <~7e-4 (<~4e-3 if confined to 10 km near the source). Tentative Qp/Qs ~0.56 (0.4-0.9). PB dt*_S drift +5 ms (2008-12) -> 0 after 2015, likely instrumental.
  - VERDICT: no robust secondary phase; no attenuation change at any quake, restart or long-term.
  - Disk: /home 99% full (29 GB free) on 2026-11-25; bulk outputs for new jobs go to /data/wsd02/maleen_data/.

  - Joint LFE-minus-earthquake layer dv/v (22 km to family depth; honest SE): [SUPERSEDED/WITHDRAWN (see 2026-11-25 code review)] M7 +0.001+/-0.008%, restart -0.002+/-0.018, Ferndale +0.007+/-0.006, Petrolia +0.004+/-0.006, decline -0.001+/-0.005; yearly 2010-2026 all within +/-0.008%. The precision comes from inter-family depth leverage (LFE-only with free receiver terms gives the same +/-0.008). Earthquake-contrast-only route: M7 -0.020+/-0.037, restart +0.007+/-0.061, Ferndale +0.004+/-0.023, Petrolia -0.041+/-0.026, decline -0.028+/-0.015 (1.9 sigma, chance among 5). Consistent with deep -0.002+/-0.009%; 10-20x tighter than near-source S-P (~0.3%).   - So: LFEs strongly tidal; repeaters show no detectable tidal dependence, with 95% limit ~+/-12-16% rate modulation (cannot exclude the few-% modulation typical of earthquakes). Astronomical-phase test only (no stress model). Per-cluster (phase-free) estimates too noisy.
- 2026-11-22 FINAL location reminder: 40.050N -123.561W, 30.5 km (likely ~28 km after depth-bias correction), 0.59 km from Shelly.
- 2026-11-22 FINDING (from family_report on family 23): bin trend since 2020 in depth = -2.0 +/- 1.5 m/yr (only ~1.3 sigma); the 'shallowing 15-25 m since 2020' should be called marginal.
- 2026-11-22 dv/v transient re-fit with script for family 23 (tau fixed at 60 d): A = -0.067 +/- 0.022% (-3.1 sigma), BUT only 3 bins fall within 120 d after the M7.

## Findings: quake matched-filter scans, Petrolia / Ferndale / M7 (2026-11-23, item 6)
- Setup: all 27 families, era templates (scripts/scan_quakes_templates.sh: events from ~3 months before each quake, >= last 300), one-pass multi-family scanner scripts/scan_quakes_mf.py (same CC/peaks as scan_matched_filter.py; verified identical on fam23 2024-11-20). Windows: Petrolia 2021-11-10..2022-01-19 (pre 30 d / post 30 d), Ferndale 2022-11-20..2023-01-19, M7 rescanned 2024-11-10..2025-01-04 (pre 25.8 d). Two channel configurations: all channels, and a FIXED channel set (channels with data on >=90% of days both before and after; 48 / 39 / 61 channels; scripts/scan_quakes_chanset.py). Fixed set is primary (BK.RVIT and PB boreholes come and go).
- Rules: daily 8xMAD (standard), hourly 8xMAD, fixed absolute CC-sum thresholds 8/10/12 x b_F (b_F = pre-quake median hourly MAD of family F). Cross-family dedup +-2 s (max meancc). False-alarm control = reversed-polarity detections not within 3 s of a catalog LFE or catalog-matched positive. Aftershock-coda flag: an NCSN quake 15-65 s before a detection.
- Recall of pre-quake catalog events (any family template, daily rule; fixed set / all): Petrolia 0.59/0.65, Ferndale 0.49/0.54, M7 0.74/0.79 (fixed10: 0.38-0.61). Few genuinely new events: non-catalog positives ~ reversed false alarms; coherence check (check_reversed.py style) shows post-quake new positives are noise-like (M7 0.64/0.24, Ferndale 0.68/0.25 frac-same-sign/median|CC|, = reversed FAs) while post-quake catalog-matched ones look real (Ferndale 0.74/0.33). Petrolia coherence groups are indistinguishable (metric not discriminating for 2021 channels).
- THRESHOLD: the MAD-based (adaptive) threshold hardly moves after any quake (hourly MAD median 0.92-1.09 of pre, max 1.22) although 2-8 Hz channel RMS rises 16-35x in the first day and 4-16x over 2 d. Normalised CC is amplitude-blind, so detection loss is not via the threshold but via SNR. Measured directly by INJECTION (scripts/scan_quakes_inject.py: typical catalog LFEs, 30-70th pct strength, raw donor segments added to continuous data, 12 per family per day, 25-26 days per quake incl. pre days; 8,100-8,424 injections per quake).
- Relative detection efficiency (daily rule, typical / strong donors): first 6 h ~0.1-0.2; first 24 h 0.4-0.6; day 1-2 ~0.6-0.9; Petrolia and M7 back to 0.8-1.0 by day 3-10. FERNDALE: efficiency falls again to 0.05-0.35 on days 7 and 15-25 (late-Dec 2022 / Jan 2023 atmospheric-river storms + aftershocks incl. 2023-01-01): mean over 30 d only 0.35-0.48.
- Catalog aftershock contamination: 20 of the 23 Ferndale catalog "LFEs" in the first 24 h follow an NCSN earthquake by 15-65 s (87% vs 13% for random times): mostly aftershock-coda false detections. Clean counts: Ferndale 24 h ~3.5 (ratio 0.08, 3 families), 2 d 27 (0.32, 14 families); Petrolia 24 h ~2 (0.05), 2 d 12 (0.12, 6 families).
- Rate ratios (post/pre; fixed set, fixed 10x CC-sum threshold FA-corrected | catalog | efficiency-corrected scan / clean catalog [p empirical vs pre-quake sliding windows, clustering-aware]):
  - Petrolia: 24 h 0.0 (0 det) | 0.08 | 0-0.8 / 0.1-0.2 [scan 0.14-0.6, cat 0.15-0.3]; 2 d 0.08 | 0.13 | 0.13-0.3 / 0.16-0.3 [0.06-0.18]; 30 d 0.71 | 0.94 | 0.76-1.0 / 0.98-1.3.
  - Ferndale: 24 h 0.0 | 0.53 (clean 0.08) | 0 / 0.1-0.19 [scan 0.07-0.14, cat 0.04-0.14]; 2 d 0.63 | 0.59 (clean 0.32) | 0.3-0.7 / 0.34-0.46 [0.07-0.39]; 30 d 0.35 | 0.54 | 0.86-1.0 (all-ch 1.3-1.5) / 1.1-1.7.
  - M7: 24 h 0 (net <= 0) | 0 | 0 / 0 [0.03-0.11; 0.05]; 2 d 0 | 0 | 0 / 0 [0.00-0.03]; day 2-7: expected 72-133 detections, observed net 0-10 (p 0); 30 d 0.37 | 0.43 | 0.45-0.6 / 0.5-0.7.
- VERDICT: M7 = real cessation (survives a ~40-50% first-day and ~25% day 2-7 efficiency loss with p <~0.03). Petrolia = brief 1-2 d deficit, about half of which is detection loss; residual (~0.2-0.3 of normal over 2 d) is within LFE burstiness (p 0.06-0.2); 30 d unchanged. Ferndale = first-day catalog count dominated by aftershock-coda false detections; genuine LFEs show a 1-2 d deficit similar to Petrolia (marginal, p ~0.04-0.14); the sustained ~50% 30-d "drop" is detection loss (storm + aftershock noise), efficiency-corrected 30-d ratio ~0.9-1.3. => Only the M7 produced a statistically robust, multi-day LFE cessation. Lu, Ide & Ren's "ceased after each of three" is not supported for Petrolia/Ferndale at LFE level; their 30-d Ferndale ratio is likely biased by the same winter-storm noise (their noise check is for the M7 only).
- Caveats: injected "typical" donors sit near the scan threshold; strong-donor (population-matched) efficiencies give the same picture (scripts/scan_quakes_popeff.py). Efficiency correction assumes the catalog loses detections like our daily-rule scan. LFE clustering makes 1-2 d windows weak (pre-quake 24-h windows with zero net detections occur ~10-14% of the time). Fixed-set sensitivity is lower than the catalog's (typical LFE ~7xb_F < 10xb_F). A catalog-only Poisson regression on noise was confounded and is not used (data/scan_quakes/noise_glm.csv). Extension downloads (Petrolia 2022-01-20..02-08, Ferndale 2023-01-20..02-08, ~16 GB in data/continuous) were not scanned.
- Outputs: data/scan_quakes/{petrolia,ferndale,m7}{_fc,}/ (det_/hourly_/chanrms_ per day), *_inj/ (injection runs), inject/*_inject*.pkl, {tag}_summary.json, {tag}_family_table.csv (per family recall/new/rates), {tag}_hourly_threshold.csv, {tag}_detections.csv, {tag}_injection.csv, {tag}_popeff.json, {tag}_coherence.csv, ncsn_{quake}.xml, chanset_{quake}.txt, summary_three_quakes.csv. Figures: figures/sci_scan_quakes_{threshold,rates,ratios}{_fc,}.png. Logs logs/scan_quakes_*.log.

Even if wholly at 10-30 km: <= ~0.14%. Conclusion: no deep change; network-series dip is mostly composition + source asymmetry.

- 2026-11-25 16:35 (true clock; the coordinator's earlier log times today of ~16:15/~16:40/~17:30/~18:00 were estimates and are ~1-1.5 h too late). - RESULTS (honest SE = max(formal, bootstrap, formal x placebo z-RMS); t with ~4-7 dof):
  1. Deep terms, stage 1, PRIMARY clean subset (take-off cos>=0.2, no lateral exit; 18.8k rows; free receiver terms + origin/location nuisances):
     - Layer contrast 3-D: M7 -0.13+/-0.27, Petrolia -0.05+/-0.40, Ferndale +0.39+/-0.50, D -0.11+/-0.45. 1-D: M7 -0.20+/-0.25, Petrolia -0.56+/-0.32 (p 0.15), D -0.51+/-0.36. Absolute layer (with regional terms) M7 -1.15+/-0.94 (3-D).
     - 20-km box 3-D M7 +0.10+/-0.16, 1-D -0.03+/-0.14. 10-km spheres 3-D M7 -0.05+/-0.19, Ferndale +0.70+/-0.33 (p 0.08). 5-km fault slab 3-D M7 +0.02+/-0.11, D +0.13+/-0.12; 1-D slab M7 +0.02+/-0.10.
     - Regional 1-10 / 10-20 km unconstrained (+/-0.5-2.3%). 2-sigma detectable: layer contrast ~0.5-1%, box/sphere ~0.3-0.5%, slab ~0.2-0.25%; a -0.1% deep change is NOT testable.
     - Look-elsewhere: 360 deep tests, 5 at p<0.05 (18 expected); clean subset 1/232.
  2. Design capability: free receiver/nuisance terms inflate SE 50-100x (layer/box/sphere), 14x slab; clean cross-geometry recovery 0.8-1.1 (all rows 0.38-0.54); layer contrast leakage: -0.1% at 10-20 km -> +0.064-0.069; 57% of a box change appears in the layer term; shallow/clock leakage <=0.01% (stage 1).
  3. Honest direct-S (everything below 1 km, stage-1 style): M7 +0.06+/-0.53%, D +0.24+/-0.34% (earlier +/-0.009% withdrawn).
  4. Petrolia/decline layer lead NOT robust: 1-D vs 3-D clean disagree (-0.56 vs -0.05), S-only flips sign, jackknife z~-1, yearly series = reversal of a pre-2021 drift rather than a step.
  5. LP 0.05-0.1 Hz >=80 km Petrolia/D -0.10 FAILS checks: station-cluster bootstrap z -1.4/-1.3; ZZ -0.22 vs TT +0.05; causal -0.14 vs acausal +0.03 (noise-source); azimuth-dependent; excluded from deep terms.
  6. Shallow-only model fits all data types (deep terms d-chi2 <=6.7 for 3 params). Stage 2 (conditional): coda coupling factor at grid edge (SC 10, LFE 5, repeaters 2.5), so coda needs 2.5-10x the kernels' near-surface sensitivity; deep-coda nuisance u required (d-chi2 21-55); SC chi2/n 2.1 at M7. M7 station terms negative almost everywhere (z<-2: 14/38 at 0-0.1 km, 16 at 0.1-1, 23 at 1-3 km, partly prior/coupling driven).
  7. Attribution components mapped to depth (IVW network mean, 0-0.1/0.1-1/1-3 km): annual amplitude 0.109+/-0.004/0.059+/-0.002/0.012+/-0.004 (mostly top 100 m); M7 initial drop -0.28/-0.20/-0.22; M7 level after 1 yr -0.14/-0.04/-0.07; Petrolia drop -0.14/-0.18/-0.01; Ferndale -0.23/-0.19/+0.04; 2021->2025 total (10 identifiable stations) -0.12/-0.10/-0.09. Caveat: amplitudes scale with the coupling factor; depth split of quake drops not robust given the kernel misfit.

  - (a) 13-quake test of their mechanism: only the M7 produces an undrained pore-pressure drop at the LFEs (-2.3 kPa), and only the M7 silenced them. Petrolia 1.32x and Ferndale 0.52x; dCFS and clamping alone do not single out the M7.

Time to the first post-M7 event vs patch length L1 (rho +0.14, p 0.53), patch area (+0.26, p 0.22), time since the last pre-M7 event (+0.25, p 0.21), 30-d pre-rate (-0.29, p 0.14), longitude (+0.04, p 0.87), depth (+0.17, p 0.43): all NOT significant. Time to the 10th event is controlled by the background rate (rho -0.78 vs 30-d rate, +0.76 vs median wait), which is trivial. std_strike vs tenth p 0.033 is not significant after 16 tests. => Neither patch size nor proximity to failure explains the variation, and there is no W-E trend. This agrees with our earlier "restart synchronous, no spatial organisation" result and disagrees mildly with S&M's suggested W-to-E restart migration.
  - (c) Their dynamic-triggering caveat: we have the remote/local triggering analysis (saturating at ~0.6 kPa; Petrolia 173 kPa dynamic stress raised the rate, while the M7 229 kPa silenced it), plus strain (<Mw 4.4 per episode) and GNSS (<Mw 5.5-5.6 at 3-90 d) bounds on triggered slip, and the moment deficit repaid in 60-90 d.
  - (d) Tidal test of their friction claim: the SPOTL agent was asked to fit the tides with the same poroelastic form (B, intrinsic mu grid), testing whether one model (B ~1, mu ~0.6) explains both the tides and the M7.
  - (e) Not in S&M at all: 18-yr LFE-coda dv/v plus ambient-noise confirmation, resolved patch sizes, inter-family coupling and sweeps, the annual LFE-rate cycle, the remote-triggering threshold, moment repayment, no healing.

  - Coulomb (Coulomb 3.3, mu' = 0.2, receiver 284/76/177): the cluster is in POSITIVE dCFS (also at mu' 0 and 0.4) and in positive dilatation (near a nodal line). Robust across source and receiver models.
  - Recovery lasted ~1 month and migrated E -> W. Their model: coseismic dilatancy, permeability increase and drainage, then fluid recharge. They claim the first report of a tremor decrease after large earthquakes.
- 2026-11-23 CHECK of their "each of three quakes" claim against the LFE catalog (daily counts, 2-d bins):
  - Petrolia: only 4 LFEs in the first 24 h; only 8 of 27 families active in the first 2 d; 26-27 families active within 2-7 d. 30-d count 1374 vs 1459 before (unchanged; our earlier 1.32x used other windows). => A ~1-2 day dip, which could be aftershock detection loss, then normal. NOT a cessation.
  - Ferndale: 23 LFEs in the first 24 h; 20 families active in 0-2 d; all within 2-7 d. 30-d count 695 vs 1295 (~0.5x). => A sustained ~50% rate reduction, NOT a cessation.
  - M7: 0 in 24 h; 0 families in 0-2 d, 1 in 2-7 d, 4 in 7-14 d, 27 in 14-30 d. => true cessation.
  - So the LFE data show graded responses (Petrolia ~none or a brief dip, Ferndale halved in the catalogue [CORRECTED 2026-11-23 by our matched-filter scan: the 30-d halving is detection loss from storms and aftershocks; genuine LFEs show only a marginal 1-2 d deficit], M7 shut off for 7-25 d), not "ceased after each". This is consistent with our dose-response result that only the M7 produces a pore-pressure drop at the LFEs.
  - Whether the Petrolia/Ferndale first-day dips are real or detection loss is exactly what the paused matched-filter scan (item 6) tests. Its priority is now raised: run it after items 1-5.

  - Friction: S&M 0.6 vs LIR 0.1. Our SPOTL poroelastic tidal fit is pending.

## Methods advice (Fable agent)
Partly verified, rest from memory.
- Velocity: Furlong 2024 primary; Guo et al. 2021 (+2025 update, not public) alternative; run 1D baseline; station statics from nearby seismicity matter more than 1D vs 3D.
- Absolute location:
  - Master stack from top 10-20% CC detections; pick S on horizontals, P on verticals (P marginal); S unc 0.05-0.1 s, P 0.1-0.2 s; EDT likelihood.
  - Expected +/-1-2 km horizontal, +/-2-3 km depth; watch shear-wave splitting and PB borehole horizontal orientations.
  - Validate by locating nearby catalog earthquakes with the same grids.
  - Pass criterion: within ~2 km of Shelly's centroid (40.0534, -123.5561, 27 km).
- Relative relocation: no pairwise HypoDD on 26k low-SNR events.
  - Tier 1: sub-stacks (100-200 events, equal-count bins) vs master.
  - Tier 2: individual event vs template S dt (2-8 Hz, S -0.5..+2.5 s, max lag +/-0.2 s, CC>=0.5 (0.4 boreholes), >=6 stations, gap <180 deg); expect ~5-15% of events relocatable at 200-500 m.
  - GrowClust/hypoDD only on the well-linked subset.
- dv/v: no established LFE-family dv/v precedent; repeater precedents: Poupinet 1984, Schaff & Beroza 2004, Hotovec-Ellis 2022 (Kilauea), USGS CWIRE.
  - Use stacks (~200-event bins, edges at Dec 2021/2022/2024 quakes), all-pairs + CWIRE-style inversion; coda S+3 s to SNR~2; 2-8 Hz (also 2-4, 4-8); stretching or MWCS with free intercept.
  - Separate clock error (constant dt), source shift (azimuth-dependent, no lapse-time slope), velocity change (dt proportional to lapse time).
  - Use isolated detections (no other-family detection within +/-15 s); fixed core station set; never span instrument changes (KBS/KBSB, KCPB/KCPC separate).
  - Expected ~0.05-0.1% resolution; coseismic drops likely <=0.05%.
  - Null tests: pre-event noise window, shuffled bins, synthetic 0.1% stretch injection, odd/even split halves.
- Sequencing: (1) alignment QC (stack S time vs year per station), (2) master stack + 1D/3D location vs Shelly, (3) sub-stack relocation on fixed station subset, (4) dv/v nulls, (5) scale to 27 families.
- 2026-11-23 ~17:45 Source-receiver dv/v separation launched at the user's request ("can't we see deep velocity changes?"). Script scripts/sci_dvv_source_receiver.py, outputs data/sci/dvv_source_receiver/ (done 2026-11-23; see Findings: family source terms null, common deep term bounded only). Fable review (from memory, no literature check; verify citations):
  - Family deviations S_F - mean(S) are identifiable. The common deep term is NOT identifiable (null space; stretching about S cancels the common ballistic leg). Lapse-time and borehole-vs-surface constraints are weak; borehole coda is dominated by free-surface ghosts (KiK-net literature), which may explain why the 412-592 m sensors decline like the 175-218 m ones. HAYF/common-mode gives only an upper bound on w_S * S_bar. Source-side kernel weight w_S is plausibly 1-10%.
  - Detectability: stress sensitivity of velocity at LFE-zone effective stress (~0.1-1 MPa) is ~1e-8 to 1e-7 /Pa. The M7 static 2.3 kPa then gives local dv/v 2e-5 to 2e-4, diluted by w_S => undetectable. Detectable are local changes of ~0.1-1% (MPa-scale pore-pressure transients, damage, or dynamic stresses of 0.1-1 MPa at the LFEs).
  - Ranked recommendations: (1) primary test = family deviations at the M7 and during the silence vs per-family silence duration and modelled stress; (2) independent receiver curves from ordinary repeating earthquakes nearby with matched kernels (the only clean route to the common deep term); (3) measure w_S from inter-family coda coherence vs lapse time; (4) search the stacks for source-side phases (LVZ reverberations, S-to-P conversions; cf. Nowack & Bostock 2013) and track their delay relative to S; (5) fixed time-after-S windows, joint shift+stretch fit, balanced panel of long-record channels; (6) borehole ghost autocorrelation and strainmeters to settle the depth of the decline; (7) HAYF / common-mode bound with w_S; (8) lapse-time kernels and inter-family interferometry as secondary. The post-2021 decline remains receiver-side unless (2) shows otherwise.
  - Items 1, 3, 5 (partly) and 7 were relayed to the running source-receiver agent. Candidates for later: 2, 4, 6.


## Decisions
- 2026-11-21 Use the v2 catalog only (user correction). Analyses filter `source != 'v1_only'` in the merged file (v2 times/families unchanged there).
- 2026-11-21 Start with family 23 (see Waveform download); family 27 optional.
- 2026-11-21 Candidate stations = 65 km superset (48 stations); measure per-station detection ourselves.
- 2026-11-21 Waveform windows -15 s to +45 s; one instrument per station per day.
- 2026-11-21 Primary velocity model: Furlong 2024 3D, with a 1D baseline to follow.
- 2026-11-21 Do not use the base conda env; use `lfe`.
- 2026-11-21 DECISION: use 3D + earthquake statics as the primary family location; report 1D as baseline.
- 2026-11-21 DECISION: station detection defined as CC > per-station 99th percentile of noise-window CC (cc_null; 1% false-alarm rate), thresholds ~0.56-0.72; NC.KPP 0.95 (monochromatic hum correlates with everything, effectively unusable).
- 2026-11-21 DECISION: fixed-station CORE set = PB.B045, B046, B047, B049, B932, B933, B934, B935, NC.KMR, KMPB, KBN, KFP, KIP, KCR, KSM (KSM P only).
- 2026-11-21 DECISION: for dv/v the vs-reference series is primary; the all-pairs inversion is a cross-check (unstable with half-size bins).
- 2026-11-21 USER CHOICE: pursue Fable ideas 1, 3, 5, 6, "only for silent period", interpreted as the post-M7 LFE shutdown (2024-12-05 18:44 UTC to ~2024-12-28; family 23: last LFE 16 h before M7, next 10.3 d after, sporadic until full restart ~23 d after).
  - (1) Healing: restart events (first ~500 from 2024-12-16) vs last ~500 pre-M7; relative amplitude vs master per station (scale factor), envelope duration, spectral ratio.
  - (3) Vp/Vs: pre-M7 vs restart stacks, per-station change in S-P time (pore-pressure drop predicts lower Vp/Vs).
  - (5) Rake stability: P/S and SH/SV amplitude ratios of pre vs restart stacks (horizontals rotated to radial/transverse; PB borehole orientations from metadata).
  - (6) Matched-filter scan of CONTINUOUS data during the silence (2024-12-05..12-28) with the family-23 template, both positive and reversed polarity: did the family stop or just fall below catalog threshold? Any reverse slip? Calibration window = weeks before the M7 (must recover catalog events; reversed polarity sets false-alarm level).

- 2026-11-22 The user told me to stop guessing what CWIRE does and to get Fable's advice. Downloaded the actual USGS CWIRE 1.0.0 source (Hotovec-Ellis, CC0; DOI 10.5066/P13BKSJJ; code.usgs.gov/vsc/seis/tools/cwire) to the session scratchpad. From inversion.py: damped LSQ (LSQR) for dv/v at evenly spaced control points; pair times enter the A matrix by linear interpolation; a mean row; 1st- and 2nd-derivative regularization in a 0.5/0.5 ratio; lambda grid 5000..0.5 chosen by L-curve maximum curvature; per-segment std of misfit as the error; one inversion per station/channel; pairs filtered by ccc_vel > inversion_ccc_vel_min. A Fable agent is reviewing CWIRE versus our method; the recommendation is pending.

Launched five parallel investigations as subagents; each writes only new files, uses <=4 CPUs, includes null tests, and does not edit NOTES.md (coordinator relays results).
  1. Tidal and seasonal sensitivity through time: does M2/fortnightly tidal modulation of LFE rate change at the quakes (effective-stress / pore-pressure test of Shelly & Materna)? Also the annual LFE-rate phase vs the dv/v phase. Files: scripts/sci_tides.py, data/sci/tides/.
  2. Moments, slip budget, healing: relative S amplitudes on the long-running stations; completeness; cumulative moment deficit and catch-up after the M7/Petrolia/Ferndale; slide-hold-slide healing (first-event amplitude vs log quiet duration over 18 yr, with the M7 hold as one point); rough slip rate. Files: scripts/sci_moments.py, data/sci/moments/.
  3. Within-patch dynamics (AxB): burst-front direction inside patches vs network sweep direction; core vs halo (isolated vs in-burst, mean_cc-matched); tidal centroid shift; D^2(tau) structure function. Files: scripts/sci_patch_dynamics.py, data/sci/patch_dynamics/.
  4. Centroid kinematics with the common mode removed (core bins): separate station/velocity artefacts from differential motion; test the M7 shallowing and the post-2020 trend; dv/v-predicted apparent shift; multi-year differential migration. Files: scripts/sci_centroid_kinematics.py, data/sci/centroids/.
  5. Cascades II: episode statistics through time and around the quakes; directed interaction network; propagation of the M7 restart; remote dynamic triggering by large distant quakes (stack of M>=6.5). Files: scripts/sci_cascades2.py, data/sci/cascades2/.

  - Data: single-station SC noise 10-day backward (5 bands, 27 sta), long-period inter-station noise (~1,100 pair-band steps), LFE coda per channel (66 M7) + 25 family deviations, repeater coda deep/shallow (18), direct-S delays (22 sta), S-P near-source, LFE-earthquake 22-31 km layer value; quantities M7, Petrolia, Ferndale, 2021->2025 decline.

- 2026-11-25 ~14:40 DECISION: independent Fable review of data/sci/dvv_3d/PLAN.md. Verdict: the 9,000-cell Gaussian inversion (0.3% prior) would make the deep null a product of the prior. Changes adopted:
  - Primary = ~80-100-parameter GLS model (per-station shallow terms in 2-3 bins, regional 1-10 km, 10-20 km, 20-31 km layer + near-source volume term, nuisances incl. clocks, common mode, coda site-coupling), flat prior on deep terms, limits only from data + injection; 3-D volume for figures only (depth-dependent prior).
  - Raw LFE S/P delays ingested once (L3/L4/L5 not independent).
  - Deep-source coda kernels S-aligned differential with depth-dependent scattering; coda not to constrain below ~3 km.
  - Common post window/time model across datasets; identical decline definition.
  - Correlated errors from simultaneous placebo dates; whole fit run on placebo dates.
  - Adversarial-kernel shallow-only tests (no inverse crime), deep truths through the real estimators, jackknives, point-spread functions.
  - 0.1-5 Hz inter-station pilot stopped (not worth it for the deep question).
  - Expected defensible claim: no layer-wide 20-31 km change to ~+/-0.02%, no near-source change to ~+/-0.3%, shallow-only models fit all data.
- 2026-11-25 ~16:40 Fable code review of the attribution scripts (sci_dvv_attr_*): NO-GO as is, GO after 6 must-fixes; forwarded to the attribution agent.
  - (M1) Open-Meteo 'best' precipitation inhomogeneous (gauge/reanalysis ratio drops 0.6-0.8 from 2010-12 to 2020-22; ERA5-only re-download).
  - (M2) quake step + opposite-sign transient pairs act as hidden ramps (VIF 100-10^4) => bounded drop0 + recovered fraction, tau <=150 d, separate 'delayed post-quake change'.
  - (M3) SDs from BIC-pruned models understate up to 10x => all 16 subsets, BIC-weighted decline partition, full-model bootstrap SD (the smoke 'B045 decline -0.224 model vs -0.222 observed' is fit closure, not attribution).
  - (M4) decline not identifiable where an instrument offset sits in 2021-25 (BRIC, RVIT, BJES, KMPB, B933) => flagged/excluded.
  - (M5) '5000-d no healing' = argmin of a flat profile => profile-likelihood interval, 'unconstrained'.
  - (M6) PGV dose: BSSA14 off x3-5 vs measured, depth ignored, permanent dose staircase mimics trend => measured PGV, hypocentral distance, local-only, transient-only.
  - Trustworthy already: coseismic drops (B045 1-3 Hz M7 -0.26%, season-matched placebo rms 0.03) and existence of a hydrological/seasonal term.
- 2026-11-25 ~17:30 DECISION: focused Fable re-review of the 3-D/joint dv/v code = conditional GO after R1, R2, R3. Production launched after fixes.
  - R1: placebo calibration via per-shift z-scores (the smoke p-values came from a fallback to formal SE).
  - R2: primary deep estimator = clean subset 'cos02+noexit' in both 1-D and 3-D geometries; all-rows secondary with x2-3 calibration uncertainty.
  - R3: stage-2 deep values conditional only; stage 2 for shallow attribution.
  - Key physics: the travel-time 'layer' term is a contrast (20-31 km vs overlying crust); honest 1 sigma ~0.2% (clean subset, M7), absolute with regional terms ~0.17%; a -0.1% deep change is not testable with travel times.
  - Honest direct-S below 1 km: M7 +0.06+/-0.39%, D +0.24+/-0.27% (replaces +/-0.009%).
  - Open lead to test in production: Petrolia step on the layer term (-0.19% all-rows, not seen in the clean 3-D subset; p~0.03-0.05 before ~30 looks) and LP >=80 km 0.05-0.1 Hz Petrolia -0.10% (z -3.9): possible microseism-source / pair-composition artefacts; tests specified.
- 2026-11-25 ~18:00 DECISION: attribution code revised and re-checked; focused Fable re-check = GO (no must-fix). Production launched.
  - M1 precip: GHCN gauges gap-filled with NASA POWER/MERRA-2 x local ratio, no drift.
  - M2: sign-constrained quake step + transient, tau grid 5-120/150 d, 'delayed post-quake change' category.
  - M3: no pruning, 16-subset BIC averaging, full-model SDs.
  - M4: chg_identifiable flag.
  - M5: profile-likelihood tau intervals.
  - M6: hypocentral distance, measured PGV + site factors, transient-only dose, teleseisms dropped.
  - Reporting should-fixes applied: quake share as dBIC<6 range + identifiable 'QUAKES+ramp21' composite; recovered fraction as upper limit when interval reaches 0 (e.g. smoke M7 '18+/-13% recovered' is indistinguishable from 0); lowest-SSE sign choice; tau cap 120 d; network p conditional on selection; site factor caveat.

## Issues & fixes
- 2026-11-21 Issue: NCEDC bulk dataselect silently returns only ONE time window per channel per request (10 windows gave 1 trace). Fix: one bulk request per time window containing all channels (`fetch_fdsn` in scripts/download_windows.py).
- 2026-11-21 Issue: first stack test missed NC/BK traces due to an off-by-one sample at window end. Fix: end the stack window inside the data span.
- 2026-11-21 Issue: date-axis bug in the availability heatmap; fixed in scripts/availability_and_histogram.py.
- 2026-11-21 Issue: base conda env h5py broken (numpy mismatch). Workaround: use env `lfe`.
- 2026-11-21 Issue: download slowed to ~5 s/day with 4 workers; fixed with 8 workers (see Waveform download).
- 2026-11-21 Issue: problem channels NC.KPP..SHZ, NC.KCPB..HHN/HHE (monochromatic noise), NC.KBS..SHZ (SNR 2); exclude/flag.
- 2026-11-21 Issue (location): first-pass S picks on vertical-only SH stations (NC.KFP, NC.KCR) picked P energy (residuals ~ -8 s). Fix: two-pass (locate, then re-pick S with AIC within +/-1.5 s of predicted) and use S only from stations with horizontal components (--allow-vertical-s to override). Visual check: S on vertical-only short-period stations (KCR, KFP, KSM, KCS, KJJ) is not reliably visible.
- 2026-11-21 Caveat (location): the second S pass is guided by the first location (not fully independent); check picks visually.
- 2026-11-21 Issue (location): first pass (before fixes) showed S residuals increasing with distance (-0.6 s near, +2-3 s at 50-65 km) while P residuals were small (+/-0.5 s); suspected too-fast model Vs (S model poorly constrained). Added Vp/Vs scan (see Findings).
- 2026-11-21 BUG (fixed): pandas 3.x (lfe env) parses time strings to datetime64[us], so `.values.astype('int64')/1e9` gives values 1000x too small (made it look like 99.6% of events had a neighbour within 15 s). Fixed in scripts/dvv_family.py (epoch_s helper, unit-safe) and scripts/merge_v1_v2.py (unit-safe). The existing merged catalog was produced under base-env pandas (ns) and is correct; verified the merge reproduces identically under the lfe env. Raw v2 has no gaps <4 s (median gap 16.5 s), as expected from Shelly's 4-s duplicate removal.
- 2026-11-21 Fix: scripts/relocate_relative.py Huber weight divide-by-zero on exact fits; floor scale at 5 ms.
- 2026-11-21 Fix: pandas 3 needs pd.to_datetime(format='mixed') for times written with/without fractional seconds.
- 2026-11-21 Issue: first detection definition (CC>=0.3 AND SNR>=2) gave median 0 stations/event: single-LFE S-window SNR is ~1.0-1.2 (amplitude SNR unusable) and CC~0.3 is the noise floor for max-over-lags on a 3-s 2-8 Hz window. Fix: per-station noise-null threshold (see Decisions). Caveat: the noise window can contain an earlier LFE of the same burst, so thresholds are slightly conservative.
- 2026-11-21 Issue: stations spanning 2008-2026 (PB boreholes from late 2008) all lie W-N of family 23 (azimuthal gap 208 deg with PB + KMR + KMPB; 0 bins passed gap<180 deg). Fix: added long-running NC vertical-only stations NC.KBN (az 120), NC.KFP (166), NC.KIP (167), NC.KCR, NC.KSM (P only), giving gap ~120 deg.
- 2026-11-21 Issue (scanner): near-zero windowed variance at gaps/zero-fill gave |CC|>>1 (ccsum ~1e5) and inflated MAD. Fix: zero CC where var <1e-3 x median, clip to +/-1.
- 2026-11-21 Issue (scanner): opposite-sign side-lobes (~0.14 s, about half period of 3-5 Hz template) of real LFEs produced spurious "reversed" detections. Fix: keep only peaks that are the absolute |ccsum| extremum within +/-1 s. Even then, 3 of ~10 catalog LFEs on 2022-10-03 appeared as reversed (negative lobe stronger) when scanned with 2008-2014 templates against 2022 data (template/instrument mismatch; narrowband signal, half-period shift ~ polarity flip).
- 2026-11-21 DECISION: for the silent-period scan use era-matched templates (events from the months just before the M7) and calibrate the false-reversal rate on pre-M7 catalog events; a reversed detection is only interesting if it has no accompanying positive detection.
- 2026-11-21 Issue (silent_period.py): noise-subtracted RMS amplitudes fail for single LFEs (S SNR ~1, negative energy, log -> -15). Fix: matched-filter projection amplitudes (unbiased by noise). P-wave shifts mostly fail quality (P stacks weak; cycle skips up to +/-0.15 s).
- 2026-11-22 Issue: PB.B045..EH1 2009-2010 shifted by ~half a period (-0.145 s) while EH2 and EHZ of B045 are on time (|lag| <=4 ms) and EH1 is fine from 2011; metadata shows a single EH1 epoch (az 159 deg), so likely an undocumented polarity reversal of EH1 in 2009-2010. Not relevant to 2024 silent-period work; exclude/flip B045.EH1 before 2011 in dv/v / relocation if it matters.
- 2026-11-22 Issue: dvv_summary split-half used channel lists re-selected in each half (half-size bins, so only BK.GHOP passed); bins without errors were dropped (40 of 147) and the Petrolia step was degenerate. Fix: dvv_fit.py uses the main-run channel list for odd/even.
- 2026-11-22 Issue: the 'fixed' core network is not fixed in practice (station availability changes around Ferndale and M7), producing apparent steps in event relocation (Ferndale E +98 m, M7 N -55 m) that vanished with availability-matched station sets (see Findings).
- 2026-11-22 Fix: family_report.py gap ranking now ranks the inter-event gap that CONTAINS the quake (family 23 M7: 11.0-d gap, rank 1 since 2009) rather than quake-to-first-event (10.3 d).
- 2026-11-22 Issue/fix: sampling-rate merge failure on 2019-09-05 and 2019-09-08 (PB.B933/B934 EH*); download_windows.py now snaps near-integer sampling rates (see Waveform download).
- 2026-11-22 Issue: availability_and_histogram.py re-read ~250k mseed headers of the shared allfam_raw archive for every family (~16 min each for families 27 and 8, while the machine was at load ~120 on 48 cores from other users). Fix: header spans now cached in data/waveforms/allfam_raw_spans.parquet (built once in 10 min; written atomically); the step now takes 6 s per family. Delete the cache if the raw archive changes.
- 2026-11-22 Note: families 27 and 8 ran their histogram step before the 2019-09-05/08 re-download, so their availability tables miss those 2 days (negligible). The build step for a large family is ~60 min at the current load.
- 2026-11-22 Issue (bottleneck): build_dataset.py reads and bandpass-filters every day file of the shared allfam_raw archive once per family. Each day file holds all families' windows, so for the 24 remaining families that means 56,572 day-reads instead of 6,438 (8.8x redundant). Processes were CPU-bound (state R, ~55% CPU each under load ~100).
- 2026-11-22 Fix: new scripts/build_all_families.py processes each day once and cuts windows for all requested families. Days are handled in time-ordered blocks of 200 and appended to resizable HDF5 datasets (chunks 64xNPTS, lzf), so memory stays bounded. Output format identical to build_dataset.py; on completion writes a fam{F}.h5.done marker. Validated on Jan-Feb 2015, families 11, 9 and 5: identical event lists, channel sets and event indices, and max abs data difference 0.0 against build_dataset.py.
- 2026-11-22 Issue: noise-window control for CWIRE-style dv/v crashed on empty input (no channel passes QC); needs a guard.
- 2026-11-22 Fix: check_reversed.py now reads 150-s windows around each candidate instead of whole days: 76 s vs ~1.5 h per family, median |CC| difference 0.006. 10 of 106 family-23 candidates came back empty (to check). Reran for 8, 27, 9, 11, 1, 24.
- 2026-11-22 Bug: in run_dvv_cwire_all.sh, $PY was not exported to xargs workers; the stale broken runner instance kept running and clashed on families 13 and 16; killed.
- 2026-11-22 ~18:10 Issue: the user caught that the published report still said "Sources are smaller than our resolution". That is wrong per the AxB split-station test: patches are resolved, ~160-210 m std along strike 293 deg (~0.5-0.7 km long), thin across.
- 2026-11-22 Fix: in scripts/build_report_html.py, the Relocation finding card, the clouds figure caption, and the Methods entry (AxB method; formal errors overstate error and must not be subtracted from raw clouds) were corrected. Rebuilt and republished https://claude.ai/artifact/Vrvac77ndqzBi6v1hDdVcU (same URL).
- 2026-11-22 Lesson: when a conclusion is revised, update every derived product (report, summaries) at the same time.
- 2026-11-22 Correction to the earlier pooled dv/v interpretation ("changes are depth-dependent, surface ~3-4x borehole"): the dv/v-depth investigation attributes the coseismic steps to near-receiver shaking damage (scales with measured PGV), not depth structure.
- 2026-11-22 Correction: "dv/v trend ~ 0" (single linear trend 2008-2026) is mis-specified; yearly means show flat 2009-2021 then a decline since late 2021 (see Findings, long-term dv/v).
- 2026-11-22 Issue (methodological): overlapping LFEs (other-family LFE within +/-10 s) bias single-event relocations by ~50 m toward the overlapping family; exclude overlaps in event-level analyses.
- 2026-11-22 Correction: the rho~0.7 between normalised M7 restart delay and longitude (23:10 cross-check) is a rate-normalisation artefact (see recovery model finding); no E-W restart gradient in the restart time itself.
- 2026-11-23 Issue (method): formal/bootstrap dv/v errors understate the real scatter by 2-3x (placebo spread does not shrink with more stacks; temporally correlated, non-stationary noise). The earlier early-coda post-M7 transient (2.5 sigma by formal errors) is downgraded accordingly; earlier dv/v significances quoted in this file use formal errors and need placebo calibration.
- 2026-11-23 Correction: the 2026-11-23 01:45 claims "right-lateral mu'~0.1", "left-lateral rejected ~22 sigma" and "tides and the M7 give the same fault friction" are tentative: the ocean load is unconverged and slip sense is undecidable with it (see ocean-load follow-up).
- 2026-11-23 Correction (placebo calibration): "coseismic dv/v drops = near-receiver shaking damage" (21:45) and "long-term decline = cumulative shaking damage" (22:30) are downgraded to plausible/open: step-vs-PGV scaling is fragile (6 long-record stations: p=0.15) and decline-vs-PGV is not significant vs placebo. The decline itself (surface -0.107%, borehole -0.057%) and the annual cycle are robust. Pooled formal SEs are unusable (placebo 1.5-3.5x).
- 2026-11-23 Issue (method): the pooled per-station dv/v curves are regularised (smoothed) except at quake dates, so quake steps look ~8x too significant vs placebo; z-values from the pooled curves (steps, timing, long-term decline z -10.7/-18) must be revised down (unsmoothed monthly refit: surface z -7.4, borehole z -5.8).
- 2026-11-23 Correction: "declined between Ferndale and the M7" and "kept declining after each quake" (22:10, 22:30 entries) are NOT supported by the decline-cause analysis (inter-quake slopes n.s.).
- 2026-11-22 Issue (correction): the "5-8x detection rise since 2019" stated in briefs was wrong (applies to good relocated events, not catalog detections; v2 counts are flat at ~9-16k/yr). Briefs given to subagents carried the wrong claim; results using it should be checked.
- 2026-11-22 Issue (provenance): the 293/40 NE right-lateral fault geometry used in all scripts was entered by the main agent from memory of Shelly et al. papers in the first Fable brief; it has NOT been verified against the paper text. Independent checks: centroid plane (ours) dip 45 toward 28 => strike ~298; Shelly catalog locations dip 46 toward 24 => ~294; pooled patch plane ~286; tidal strike scan peak ~290. Conclusions unchanged across 283-298. Plan: label 293 as literature value pending PDF check. Positive along-strike = toward azimuth 293 (WNW); down-dip = toward ~23 (NNE).
- 2026-11-22 Issue: the strain-stack agent's wait loop never exited because pgrep matched its own command line (fixed by killing the loops).
- 2026-11-22 Correction: "the along-strike sign is the same in every script" is wrong. scripts/sci_patch_dynamics.py uses the pooled patch axis at az 102, so its + along-strike points ESE (line 37), the opposite of the other scripts. scripts/sci_transient_source_common.py uses strike 290 / dip 45 (+ WNW). All others use 293 with + WNW. The "west-led/east-led" burst counts in the patch_dynamics summary are labelled with explicit geographic sides, so they are unaffected.
- 2026-11-23 ~09:00 Issue: the user had uploaded Shelly & Materna 2026 GRL (doi 10.1029/2026GL126322) on 2026-11-21 into the session uploads. It was missed and the paper was repeatedly reported as unavailable. It is now at papers/Shelly_Materna_2026_GRL.pdf, with text in papers/Shelly_Materna_2026_GRL.txt. Strike 293 / dip 40 is now confirmed by it (supersedes the provenance issue for the receiver geometry; the Science paper itself is still not read).
- 2026-11-23 Correction: the ~03:00 ocean-load note (slip sense undecidable, mu' and a*sigma unconverged, sigma_eff reading from tides) is SUPERSEDED by the SPOTL layered-Earth refit (~12:00): right-lateral decisive, mu' 0.092+/-0.013, a*sigma 0.63 kPa (rigidity-dependent); tidal a*sigma no longer ~0.46 and the "tides and M7 give the same friction" statement is replaced by: no single static (B, mu) explains both.
- 2026-11-23 Correction: the ~13:00 statement "LFEs lie just above the modelled plate interface, in the overriding plate / accreted material, not on the megathrust" is model-dependent: McCrory's interface coincides with the Bloch 2023 slab Moho, and relative to Bloch's top of the low-velocity layer the LFEs lie 3.3-8.0 km below (inside the layered package above the Moho). See the 13:30 finding.
- 2026-11-23 Issue/fix: the source-spectra analyse stage deadlocked (fork-pool worker for family 6 hung at load; parent waited on imap_unordered with 0% CPU for 2 h). Main agent killed it at ~11:45. The script now runs in-process at --procs 1, otherwise a spawn-context pool with a 1-h per-family limit.
- 2026-11-23 Correction (instrument check): "surface decline ~2x borehole" -> only ~1.2-1.5x once the instrument breaks get free offsets.
- 2026-11-23 Correction (instrument check): "BRIC noise decline -0.17% (1-4 Hz)" -> fails with a reinstall offset; only the 2-8 Hz decline and the M7 drop are robust.
- 2026-11-23 Correction (instrument check): "B933 P +13.5 ms at the M7" (centroids) -> FAILS: driven by one family (22, +219 ms, a cycle skip; +2.3 +/- 3.3 ms without it), and the whole post-M7 window lies inside B933's degraded period.
