- 2026-11-25 ~16:15 IMPORTANT CORRECTION (independent Fable code review of the joint 3-D dv/v code; NO-GO pending fixes). Affected earlier entries are annotated "SUPERSEDED/WITHDRAWN (see 2026-11-25 code review)": EARTHQUAKES ABOVE (joint layer results), DEEP VS SHALLOW (direct-S deep estimate), DEPTH SUMMARY.
  - scripts/sci_eq_above_diff.py:70-78 layer kernels use straight rays with cos i clipped at 0.05 and K=L*|g| fallback => layer time exceeds total travel time on 259/1296 LFE family-station S rows (verified by coordinator; e.g. HAYF/KMPB/B046 K_S ~40 s vs T_S ~19-20 s). The +/-0.008% (M7) / +/-0.005% (decline) 22-31 km layer precision is an artefact => WITHDRAWN. Honest travel-time numbers with free receiver terms (reviewer, placebo RMS, M7, 20-31 km layer): lay-only 1-D rays 0.124% (1 sigma), with R1_10/R10_20 terms 0.166% => 95% ~+/-0.25-0.35%. Stage-1 free-receiver-term estimate -0.176 +/- 0.132%.
  - The 2026-11-23 direct-S deep estimate (-0.002 +/- 0.009%) came from a joint fit with coda that ties receiver terms to shallow coda assumptions (same issue as review M2) => UNDER RE-CHECK; free-receiver-term version being computed by the 3-D agent.
  - Other review must-fixes for the 3-D code: M2 stage-2 deep estimate pinned by shallow priors (primary deep number = stage-1 free receiver terms + placebo SE); M3 coda forced to zero below 3 km (add fdeep nuisance); M4 SC covariance invalid (factor model). Plus should-fixes (w_final overwrite, chi2r dof, placebo scaling, secular term, box centre, jackknife estimator, kappa handling, LP mapping, codafit placebo labels).
  - Report v42 published with the correction (headline bullet, depth lede, earthquakes-above card, deep-vs-shallow card, depth summary table/figure: LFE-eq row now ~+/-0.25-0.35% 'weak limit', direct-S row 'under re-check'); GitHub synced.
  - Consequence (refined): the source-region S-P bound itself (within 10 km of the LFEs +/-0.3-0.4%, 95%; Vp/Vs +0.06 +/- 0.14%) is NOT affected; it uses its own fit with origin/location terms and placebo errors. Only statements that COMBINE it with the +/-0.02% direct-S bound (e.g. 'combined with direct S: no detectable change anywhere at depth to +/-0.02%') are affected, as is the 'deep crust unchanged to ~+/-0.02%' phrasing in the 2026-11-23 deep-vs-shallow conclusion (and the Current status Round 4 line). The repeater deep-vs-shallow difference (+0.006 +/- 0.006%) is a different quantity and stands.
  - Attribution agent (scripts/sci_dvv_attr_{download,drivers,lib,fit,figs}.py; data/sci/dvv_attr/PLAN.md; bulk /data/wsd02/maleen_data/dvv_attr/) finished code + smoke tests (ComCat 630 events; GHCN 128 stations + ERA5 gap-fill; no continuous groundwater wells -> river streamflow proxy; GNSS loading/strain; borehole areal strain), stopped for code review (running).

Order of entries is correct; absolute times are approximate.

## Files & scripts
- Catalogs: data/shelly2025_MTJ_v1/, data/shelly2026_MTJ_v2/, data/merged/LFEcat_MTJ_merged_v1v2.csv, data/merged/family_time_offsets_v1_minus_v2.csv
- Stations: data/stations/, data/stations/candidate_channels_65km_2008_2026.csv
- Waveforms: data/waveforms/fam23_raw/ (in progress), data/waveforms/fam23_availability.parquet, logs/download_fam23.log
- Velocity: data/velocity/furlong2024/, data/velocity/tt_3d/{NET.STA}.npz + grid.json
- Figures: figures/fam23_repeats_through_time.png
- Scripts: scripts/merge_v1_v2.py, scripts/download_windows.py, scripts/availability_and_histogram.py, scripts/traveltimes_3d.py (--model 3d|1d)
- Travel times: data/velocity/tt_1d/ ; logs: logs/tt_3d.log, logs/tt_1d.log
- Local PNWstore index: /data/wsd01/PNWstore_sqlite/{year}.sqlite (read-only, other users' drive)
- scripts/build_dataset.py: v2 events only; cuts -15..+45 s, demean/detrend, taper, bandpass 1-15 Hz, resample to 50 sps, float32 -> data/waveforms/fam{F}.h5 (/events, /chan/{id}/data + ev index). Response NOT removed (relative measurements; instruments kept separate per channel). Test on first 418 days: 43 channels, 82,405 traces in 48 s (16 procs) -> data/waveforms/fam23_test.h5.
- scripts/templates.py: master stack per channel (2-8 Hz; seed = top 20% by v2 ccsum, RMS-normalised; 2 iterations of CC alignment +/-0.3 s, keep best <=50% with CC>=0.1) + yearly catalog-aligned stacks vs master (lag/CC -> data/templates/fam{F}_yearly_qc.csv, figures/fam{F}_yearly_lag_qc.png).
- scripts/locate_family.py: automatic picks on master stacks (S: summed horizontal envelope peak + AIC onset; P: vertical AIC in window from predicted S-P; sigma from SNR) + L1 grid search with free origin time over tt sub-volume, bootstrap uncertainties. Outputs data/locations/fam{F}_{model}{tag}.json, _picks.csv, _residuals.csv, figures/fam{F}_{model}{tag}_location.png.
- scripts/relocate_relative.py: relative relocation vs master stacks. dt per station-channel by CC in phase windows around master picks (S -0.5..+2.5 s on horizontals, P -0.3..+1.2 s on verticals; max lag +/-0.25 s; parabolic sub-sample), linearised dt_i = grad T_i(x0).dx + dt0 using 3D tt-grid gradients (S scaled by s_scale), weighted LSQ (CC^2/sigma^2) with Huber IRLS + bootstrap. Modes: bins (equal-count consecutive events, default 150) and events (CC>=0.5, >=8 obs, >=6 stations, gap<180 deg).
- scripts/dvv_family.py: coda dv/v from isolated-LFE bin stacks (isolated = no detection of any family within +/-15 s in full v2 catalog); equal-count bins with edges forced at Dec 2021 M6.1, Dec 2022 M6.4, Dec 2024 M7.0; stretching in coda window [S+3 s, where master envelope < 2x noise], lapse time from located origin; all-pairs (<=12 bins apart) CWIRE-style inversion + vs full-period reference; direct-S constant-shift dt as clock/timing control; controls: same on pre-event noise window, synthetic -0.1% stretch injected into second half of bins.
- scripts/templates.py update: yearly QC now uses parabolic sub-sample lags; plot scale +/-0.1 s.
- scripts/validate_location_eq.py: relocates NCSN earthquakes with our grids; outputs in data/validation/ (ncsn_events_box.txt, quakeml/, station_residuals_{3d,1d}.csv). scripts/locate_family.py now has --statics.
- scripts/plot_relocations.py: bin centroid vs time + event map/along-strike view (strike 293 deg).
- scripts/run_family.sh F: runs histogram -> build -> templates/QC -> location 3d & 1d with earthquake statics -> relocation bins & events -> plot -> dv/v; logs in logs/fam{F}_*.log.
- scripts/station_detections.py: per event x station (best component; horizontals for S), max CC with the station's master stack in the S window (-0.5..+2.5 s around master S pick, lag <=0.2 s) and S-window amplitude SNR; cc_null = same CC on a noise-only window (-14.5..-11.5 s). detected := cc > per-station 99th percentile of cc_null. Outputs data/detections/fam{F}_station_detections.parquet, fam{F}_station_summary.csv, figures/fam{F}_station_detections.png. Added to run_family.sh.
- scripts/relocate_relative.py: --stations option (fixed station set). run_family.sh now also runs a fixed-station bin relocation (tag _core).
- scripts/dvv_family.py: added --subset odd|even (alternate isolated events, half bin size, same time bins) for split-half errors; --fmin/--fmax sub-bands. run_family.sh now runs 2-8 Hz (main), 2-4 Hz, 4-8 Hz, odd, even.
- scripts/dvv_summary.py: network-average dv/v per bin = weighted mean (CC^2) of per-channel dv/v vs full-period reference (demeaned per channel) over channels with mean coda CC >=0.7 and noise-control scatter >=5x coda scatter; error = split-half |odd-even|/2 combined with channel standard error; also network-median direct-S dt (clock control). Output data/dvv/fam{F}_network_dvv.csv, figures/fam{F}_dvv_network.png. Added to run_family.sh.
- scripts/download_continuous.py (planned/new): one request per station-day; output data/continuous/YYYY-MM-DD/NET.STA.mseed; PNWstore only for PB 2008-2022, else NCEDC.
- scripts/scan_matched_filter.py: matched-filter scan with family master templates (per-channel 6-s windows: [S-1, S+5] s around master S pick, or [P-0.5, P+3.5] on verticals of 3-C stations); network CC sum indexed in catalog-time convention; detections at +/-8x daily MAD (positive = normal polarity, negative = reversed polarity); peaks >=4 s apart; meancc reported. Outputs data/scan/fam{F}_{day}.csv + daily summary.
- scripts/silent_period.py (Fable ideas 1, 3, 5): groups pre = last 500 v2 events before M7 (2024-12-05 18:44), restart = first 500 after, late = 500 from 2025-03-01 (dates configurable --main/--late-start/--tag). (1) per-event matched-filter amplitude = projection of S window (2-8 Hz) on the station's pre-group stack / pre median; per-event median over >=4 stations; group means with bootstrap; S-window spectral ratios restart/pre, late/pre from group stacks (1-15 Hz). (3) P and S time shifts of restart/late stacks vs pre stack (CC + parabolic, bootstrap sd) -> d(tS-tP); quality cut CC>=0.6 and sd<=20 ms. (5) P/S (Z P RMS / horizontal S RMS) and SH/SV (rotated to radial/transverse using channel azimuths from station metadata and back-azimuth) per group with bootstrap.
- scripts/templates.py: --tmin/--tmax/--out for era-matched templates (no yearly QC in that mode). scripts/scan_matched_filter.py: --templates path.
- scripts/dvv_family.py: gained --iso (isolation window, s); used with --iso 5 --nbin 150.
- scripts/dvv_fit.py: network-average dv/v fit (offset + trend + annual + steps) with split-half errors from the same channels; figures/fam23_dvv_fit_iso5_n150.png.
- scripts/check_reversed.py: checks reversed-polarity scan candidates (per-channel coherence + NCSN earthquakes); output data/scan/fam23_coherence_check.csv.
- scripts/compare_core_events.py: comparison of core vs all-station event relocations + figure figures/fam23_events_core.png; log logs/fam23_compare_core_events.log.
- data/relocations/fam23_events_3d_{core,m7set,fernset}.csv (fixed-station and availability-matched event relocations).
- scripts/run_family_full.sh F: run_family.sh + fine dv/v (iso 5, 150/bin, odd/even) + dvv_fit + dvv_transient + core event relocation + era templates + scan (Nov 10 - Jan 10) + scan_postprocess + check_reversed + silent_period (+control) + family_report. run_family.sh build step now honours PROCS.
- scripts/scan_postprocess.py: concat daily scan files, catfam/catoth flags, silence window json data/scan/fam{F}_silence_window.json. Family 23 old file kept as data/scan/fam23_scan_all_v0.csv.
- scripts/check_reversed.py generalised (per family; NCSN events cached to data/scan/ncsn_events_20241110_20250111.xml); old version kept as scripts/check_reversed_fam23_v0.py.
- scripts/dvv_transient.py -> data/dvv/fam{F}_dvv_transient_iso5_n150.json.
- scripts/family_report.py -> data/reports/fam{F}_summary.json + fam{F}_report.md (tested on family 23).
- data/families/shelly_family_locations.csv; data/velocity/{tt_3d,tt_1d} (cluster-centred), data/velocity/{tt_3d_fam23,tt_1d_fam23}; data/waveforms/allfam_raw/.
- scripts/run_all_families.sh: runs per-family workflow for all families (NPAR=2, PROCS=12); order 27 8 19 6 22 18 21 20 1 24 14 25 12 15 7 17 2 10 13 16 26 5 4 3 9 11.
- scripts/quake_response_catalog.py -> data/reports/quake_response_catalog.csv; data/catalogs/comcat_M5.5_200km_2008_2026.csv.
- scripts/run_families_smallfirst.sh: second driver, smallest families first (NPAR=2, PROCS=6); data/locks/famF per-family locks (created by run_family_full.sh); logs/run_family{F}_full_b.log. scripts/summarize_families.py: rerun at the very end.
- scripts/build_all_families.py: one-pass dataset build for many families (output = build_dataset.py format; fam{F}.h5.done marker). scripts/run_build_all.sh: driver (PROCS=20). Locks: data/locks/allfam_build, data/locks/allfam_build.finished. Log: logs/build_all_families.log.
- scripts/dvv_family.py: now writes data/dvv/fam{F}_{chan}{tag}_pairs.csv.
- scripts/dvv_invert.py: joint all-channel inversion for one dv/v curve per family. Data: pairs plus vs-reference measurements; unknowns are the bin values and a per-channel offset. Weights CC^2/(1-CC^2); pairs with CC<0.5 dropped; Huber IRLS. Optional first-difference smoothing, never applied across the quakes; 'auto' chooses it by leave-channels-out 5-fold CV. Errors: max(LSQ, bootstrap over channels). Outputs network_dvv{tag}_inv(_smooth).csv and dvv_fit{tag}_inv.csv. Added to run_family_full.sh: unsmoothed plus transient on _inv, then smooth auto.
- scripts/run_dvv_cwire_all.sh: CWIRE-style dv/v for all families. scripts/dvv_cwire_pool.py: per-station-channel pooled curves; outputs data/dvv/pool/, figures/dvv_pool_map.png, figures/dvv_pool_channels.png.
- scripts/build_report_html.py -> report/index.html + report/figs/*.jpg (artifact https://claude.ai/artifact/Vrvac77ndqzBi6v1hDdVcU). family_report.py (cwire() section) and summarize_families.py updated.
- scripts/run_split_reloc.sh + station_split_AB.json: AxB split-station relocation (azimuth-interleaved halves of 16 stations).

- 2026-11-23 ~12:10 TIME-LAPSE SHEAR-WAVE SPLITTING (scripts/sci_sws.py: meta, measure, analyze, inject; outputs data/sci/sws/: stations.csv, incidence.csv, reference_splitting.csv, station_tests.csv, network_tests.csv, family_station_steps_*.csv, family_vs_station.csv, injection_test.csv, summary.json, meas/{STA}_{family,pooled,ref}.parquet; figures figures/sci_sws_{reference,timeseries,steps}.png; logs logs/sci_sws_*.log).
  - Method: isolated LFEs (+-5 s), horizontals gain-corrected and rotated with EPOCH azimuths; monthly family stacks (split at quakes), Silver-Chan eigenvalue grid (phi 2 deg, dt 0-0.30 s at 5 ms), window S onset -0.15..+1.0 s, 2-8 and 2-5 Hz; bootstrap over events (B=40) and odd/even halves; 28 stations (8 PB, 20 NC/BK), 27 families. Station pooling = sum of noise-normalised surfaces after RE-CENTRING each family surface on its own reference minimum (so the family mix cannot move the estimate). Observables: ddt (dt along the reference phi), dphi (phi at reference dt), dpol (S polarisation; orientation monitor).
  - Incidence (Furlong 1D, Vs=Vp/1.78): JEWT 3, KMR 21, KZE 23, SPRL 24, BRIC 27, B049/B934 28-29, B933/RVIT/BLAS/HALS 29-31 deg; all others 33-36 deg (edge of the S window in this model; real low-velocity surface layers make it steeper).
  - Absolute splitting is POORLY constrained: per-family phi scatter 20-50 deg at most stations, dt 150-230 ms ~ half the dominant period (cycle-skip ambiguity), phi often ~45 deg from the S polarisation. Most consistent: BRIC 153+-21 deg/174 ms, BLAS 33+-22/231, B932 163+-29, B933 154+-31, B934 158+-44 (NNW, SAF-parallel), B047 54+-27. Borehole (139-592 m) and surface dt are the same (~150-230 ms), so whatever is measured is not concentrated in the top ~0.5 km.
  - Time-lapse precision (monthly, pooled): ddt 1-2 ms, dphi 1-2 deg at good stations (B049, KMR, KMPB, GHOP, JEWT, SPRL, PRDS); B046, B932, HAYF, BJES 5-20. Injection (KMR, B049, 6 big families, at 2016-07): +5 ms recovered as +2.7-2.9 ms (~55%); a 3 deg sensor rotation recovered as -2.96 deg in dphi and dpol with ddt unchanged.
  - INSTRUMENT FINDINGS (SWS is a sensitive horizontal-component QC): B045 EH1 2009-10 (known polarity issue: ddt -50 ms, dphi +30); B933 2017-01..2021-11 (ddt pinned at search edge, dpol +20) and again from 2024-08 (errors x15, dphi +25, FOUR MONTHS BEFORE the M7: the B933 "+13.5 ms P at M7" in the centroid work may be instrumental); B935 from 2024-05 (phi errors 4->20 deg, 7 months before the M7); BK CMG-3T-PH sites: RVIT ddt -42 ms starting exactly at Ferndale until the 2024-06-20 reinstall, BRIC degraded from Dec 2022 (sparse) until the 2024-05-21 reinstall, BJES +40 ms 2023 until the 2024-05-22 reinstall; KMR sensor swap 2025-09 / posthole 2026-03 and pre-2009-09 epochs; KMPB pre-2010-11 epoch. All excluded (free offset across each window). Metadata azimuth epochs that survive (HAYF 2025-05 az 358->0, RBOW 2024-05) show no step (|z|<=2.2).
  - RESULTS (network pooled vs 234-268 common placebo dates; z = step / placebo MAD-SD, p = empirical): ddt steps at Petrolia/Ferndale/M7 all null (2-8 Hz: -0.14/+0.02/-0.09 ms, |z|<=0.6; borehole and surface also null; 2-sigma bound ~0.5-1 ms, ~1-2 ms after injection-recovery correction). dphi steps null for all stations together (+0.03/+0.22/-0.81 deg). Surface-only M7 dphi -0.95 deg (z -3.1, p<0.03) is ONE station: BK.SPRL (weight 0.53; record starts 2023-09); without SPRL -0.43 (2-8 Hz) / -0.03 (2-5 Hz). Surface Ferndale dphi +0.78 (z 4.1 but p 0.07). 2-5 Hz ddt Petrolia -0.89/Ferndale +0.76 ms (p 0.06/0.03) = a KMPB excursion Dec 2021-Dec 2022 (also B935 dphi +4 deg same window); its end coincides with the KMPB maintenance epoch after the Feb-Oct 2023 gap; candidate only.
  - Long-term (median 2025-26 minus 2019-21 vs pre-2021 windows): null at all stations and pooled (|z|<2); the post-2021 dv/v decline has NO SWS counterpart (KMPB ddt +0.5 ms, dphi +1.1 deg; BRIC/RVIT/BJES not testable because of the instrument windows).
  - Annual: 6/19 stations have phi annual amplitude above all placebo periods (0.6-2 deg) but phases are incoherent (R 0.26 vs 0.23 random) and the stations change between bands: NO coherent annual SWS cycle (contrast: dv/v annual cycle robust).
  - Family x station: no family (source-side) effect for any quake (p_family 0.15-0.99); station effects at Ferndale/M7 dphi (p 0.012) come from single stations (BRIC post-reinstall, B933), i.e. receiver/instrument.
  - Half-splits: odd/even half-series correlate only where instrument problems exist (B933 0.79 before exclusion, RVIT 0.78); at clean stations r = -0.3..0.4, consistent with noise-dominated series.
  - VERDICT: no detectable change of fast direction or delay time at Petrolia, Ferndale or the M7, during the post-2021 dv/v decline, or seasonally, at the ~1-2 ms / ~1-2 deg level; nothing differs between borehole and surface. All large apparent SWS changes are instrument epochs (several begin before the M7).
